## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 10 can authorize capital deployment, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Signal Half-Life Enforcement (`CB-04`):** A 1H Rough Path signature has a predictive half-life of **6 to 24 hours**. Holding a 1H tactical trade into a multi-week swing without 4H/1D signature confirmation violates path-decay physics and exposes capital to higher-timeframe trend resumption.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Hierarchy of Drift:** Higher timeframes govern structural drift (`1D > 4H > 1H`), while lower timeframes govern entry precision (`1H / 15m`). Never fight a negative 4H + 1D structural vector with full 0.75% risk size.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Counter-Trend Half-Risk Rule:** If taking a high-confluence 1H reversal inflection (`CSU-10C`) at a major HTF support while 4H/1D remain negative, position risk is automatically cut in half (**0.375% maximum portfolio equity risk**) until 4H flips positive.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Gate:** If Pillar 1 is in `RISK_OFF_100_CASH`, all `CSU-10C` counter-trend entries are disabled.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 11 can authorize a spot order, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Auction Acceptance vs. Sweep Filter (`CB-01`):** In Auction Market Theory, 70%–80% of initial pokes outside a Value Area fail and rotate back to the Point of Control (POC). You must never buy a raw breakout tick; require **acceptance** (retest of VAH holding as support) or a **failed breakdown spring** (reclaim of VAL).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — HVN Magnet Trap Warning:** Entering a trade directly into an overhead High-Volume Node (HVN) or static POC offers terrible reward-to-risk because HVNs act as two-way liquidity magnets that stall momentum. Entries must launch *away* from HVNs across Low-Volume Nodes (LVNs).
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Liquidation Cascade Stand-Down (`CB-03`):** If price slices downward through VAL during a liquidation cascade (`OI dropping > -1.5%/hr`), do not place blind limit bids at VAL—wait for `CSU-11C` reclaim confirmation.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Permission Gate:** Confirm secular macro trend permission before trading upside Value Area rotations.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-11A: Look-Below-and-Fail Spring ("VAL Liquidity Sweep & Reclaim")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price breaks downward below the multi-day or session **Value Area Low (VAL)**, sweeping resting retail stop-losses sitting beneath support.
  2. **Rejection & Reclaim:** Within 1 to 4 bars (15m/1H), price fails to build volume below VAL, snaps back **inside the Value Area**, and closes firmly above **VAL**.
  3. **Order-Flow Verification:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** prints a clear bullish absorption divergence on the sweep (`passive limit bids absorbing market sells`), and Rough Path `A_P,V` inflects positive.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional buyers used the stop-loss liquidity pool below VAL to fill large spot bids at a discount without pushing price up prematurely.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Under the **80% Auction Rotation Rule**, once price re-enters and accepts inside a Value Area after a failed breakdown, it rotates across the entire distribution—first to the **POC** and then to the **Value Area High (VAH)**.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon the confirmed **15m/1H close back above VAL** (or immediate retest of VAL from above) with positive Domestic USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop strictly beneath the **failed breakdown swing wick low** (typically `0.5%–1.5%` below VAL).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at the composite **Point of Control (POC)** (the primary high-volume magnet); move stop on remaining 50% to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Close remaining 50% at the **Value Area High (VAH)** unless `CSU-11B` breakout acceptance triggers.

### CSU-11B: Low-Volume Node (LVN) Fast-Traverse Breakout ("Initiation Away From Value")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price breaks above the **Value Area High (VAH)** and enters a **Low-Volume Node (LVN)** air pocket (`volume density <= 20th percentile`).
  2. **Acceptance Verification:** Price holds above VAH for `>= 2 consecutive 15m closes` (or 1H close), Developing POC (`dPOC`) migrates upward toward the breakout zone, and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** + Rough Path `A_P,V` (`>= +1.5 Z`) surge upward.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market participants have rejected the prior lower value range and are aggressively repricing Bitcoin upward to establish a new equilibrium.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Because LVNs contain minimal historical resting liquidity, price traverses the LVN rapidly until it reaches the next overhead **High-Volume Node (HVN)** or HTF Value Area.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on a shallow 15m pullback to the **reclaimed VAH / LVN entry lip** where sellers fail to push price back into the old Value Area. Size strictly via the **0.75% Risk Formula** with hard stop just inside the old Value Area (`below VAH`).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% upon reaching the base of the next overhead **High-Volume Node (HVN)**; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind the rising 1H Developing POC (`dPOC`).

### CSU-11C: Look-Above-and-Fail ("VAH Sweep Rejection & Downward Rotation")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price pokes above the **Value Area High (VAH)**, sweeping breakout buyers and short stops.
  2. Price fails to hold above VAH, closes back **below VAH** on the 15m/1H chart, while **Domestic USD Spot CVD** diverges negatively (`Perp CVD positive, USD Spot CVD negative`) and `A_P,V <= -1.25 Z-score`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional spot sellers used the breakout liquidity above VAH to distribute inventory into late breakout chasers.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-probability rotation downward through the **POC** toward the **Value Area Low (VAL)**.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** If holding an existing Spot Long from lower inside the range, immediately execute **Stage 2 Full Profit Exit** upon the close back below VAH.

### CSU-11D: High-Volume Node (HVN) Equilibrium Chop ("POC Balance Lock")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price oscillates tightly within `+/- 0.75%` of a multi-day **High-Volume Node / Point of Control (POC)**.
  2. Developing POC (`dPOC`) remains stationary, Garman-Klass volatility compresses below the 20th percentile, and Spot/Perp CVD slopes are flat.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Two-sided institutional liquidity is in complete equilibrium; market makers are harvesting bid-ask spread while directional traders get chopped to pieces.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY / 100% CASH PRESERVATION.** Zero trades allowed inside an HVN magnet zone. Wait for price to reach an edge (`VAL` or `VAH`) and trigger `CSU-11A` or `CSU-11B`.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Why **Garman-Klass (GK)** beats standard close-to-close volatility or Bollinger Bands: GK incorporates **intraday High, Low, Open, and Close (`OHLC`)**, capturing hidden intrabar range expansion and wick rejection that close-to-close estimators miss. Before acting on Module 12, four circuit breakers apply:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — No Pre-Squeeze Guessing (`CB-01`):** A bottom-decile GK volatility squeeze (`<= 15th percentile`) guarantees an upcoming volatility expansion, **not** its direction. Guessing direction inside a squeeze violates deterministic rules; wait for confirmed VAH/VAL breakout + Domestic USD Spot CVD.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Volatility-Adjusted Position Sizing (`CB-04`):** Because `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Equity) / (Entry - Stop)`, wider GK volatility fans automatically widen your structural stop distance (`1.5x–2.0x GK expected bar range`), which **mathematically forces your BTC position size smaller** during volatile regimes—protecting you from noise stop-outs without ever increasing dollar risk above `0.75%`.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Scam-Wick Filtering (`CB-02`):** Use composite USD spot OHLC data so an isolated offshore perp liquidation wick does not falsely inflate your GK volatility fan.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Climax Exhaustion Ban (`CB-03`):** Never initiate a momentum breakout entry when GK volatility is already above the **92nd rolling percentile**; at that extreme, volatility is statistically primed to mean-revert rather than expand further.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-12A: Bottom-Decile Volatility Squeeze -> Spot-Backed Trend Birth
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Compression Phase:** Rolling Garman-Klass volatility compresses into the **`<= 15th percentile`** for `>= 24 to 72 hours` while price coils inside a tight Volume Profile Value Area.
  2. **Expansion Ignition:** Price breaks decisively above the **Value Area High (VAH)** and upper **1-Sigma GK Volatility Fan**, pushing GK volatility upward out of compression (`> 35th percentile`).
  3. **Flow & Path Confirmation:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** surges positive (`>= +1.5 Z-score`) and Rough Path `A_P,V` confirms volume-led upward curvature.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market makers and options dealers who were short gamma inside the quiet range are forced to chase spot inventory as price breaks the volatility fan, fueling a multi-day trend leg.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained directional expansion (`+5% to +15%`) as realized volatility expands from bottom decile toward upper quartile.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on the first 15m/1H pullback retest of the **reclaimed VAH / upper GK squeeze lip** (or immediately if `Mutation M2` head-fake already occurred).
  * **Position Sizing Formula (GK-Calibrated 0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Stop Distance = max(VAH_to_POC_Distance, 1.75 * GK_1H_Expected_Range)`
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / Stop Distance`
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% when price reaches the **2.5-Sigma Upper GK Volatility Fan** (`GK percentile >= 85th`); move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind the 4H VAL or lower 1-Sigma GK channel until volatility peaks and rolls over.

### CSU-12B: The Head-Fake Initial Expansion ("Stop-Hunt Squeeze Reversal")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. From a **`<= 15th percentile` GK Volatility Squeeze**, price initially breaks **downward** below VAL, sweeping obvious retail stop-losses.
  2. **Failure of Follow-Through:** Within 1 to 3 hours, Open Interest drops (`short-term longs flushed`), **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD surges positive** (absorbing the sweep), and price violently snaps back through the POC and breaks **above the squeeze VAH**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Classic liquidity-engineering maneuver: market makers expanded volatility downward just enough to clear leveraged long stops and trigger breakout shorts before reversing into the true institutional upward impulse.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Explosive upside trend expansion fueled by both institutional spot accumulation and trapped breakout shorts.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** immediately upon the **15m/1H close back above the squeeze POC/VAH**, sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling** with hard stop beneath the head-fake sweep wick low.

### CSU-12C: Top-Percentile Volatility Climax ("Mean-Reversion Exhaustion")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Garman-Klass volatility spikes into the **`>= 92nd rolling percentile`** (extreme vertical candle ranges and wide upper/lower wicks).
  2. **Upside Climax:** Price hits the **3-Sigma Upper GK Fan** while 8H perpetual funding spikes `> 90th percentile` and **Domestic USD Spot CVD flattens/diverges negatively**.
  3. **Downside Climax:** Price hits the **3-Sigma Lower GK Fan** on `> 85%` long liquidations (`MOD 03`), followed by immediate 15m limit-bid absorption.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Volatility has reached statistical exhaustion; market makers widen spreads and stop chasing directional momentum, causing price to snap back toward the session VWAP / POC.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action (Upside Climax):** **EXECUTE STAGE 2 FULL PROFIT EXIT (100% OF OPEN SPOT SWING).** Lock Cash.
  * **Action (Downside Climax):** **HOLD 100% CASH** during the spike; authorize a reduced-size (`0.375%–0.75%` risk) mean-reversion Spot Long *only* after GK volatility ticks downward for 2 consecutive 1H bars and 15m VAL is reclaimed with USD Spot CVD.

### CSU-12D: Grinding Low-Volatility Uptrend ("Quiet Institutional Drift")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price trends steadily higher over 7–21 days while Garman-Klass volatility drifts downward into the **20th–35th percentile** (small, orderly green candles with minimal wicks).
  2. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** and US Spot ETF netflows climb steadily while perpetual funding stays neutral (`+0.005% to +0.010%`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Pure institutional TWAP/VWAP spot accumulation without retail leverage mania—the healthiest possible wealth-compounding regime.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **HOLD CORE SPOT SWING POSITION.** Because GK volatility is low, tighten trailing stops closer (`1.5x GK range` below 4H VAL) to maximize capital efficiency while keeping risk locked at `<= 0.75%`.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 13 can authorize a spot decision, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Psychological Polarity Flip (`CB-03`):** Short-Term Holders (`< 155 days`) are the most price-sensitive cohort in Bitcoin. When price trades *above* STH Realized Price (`STH-MVRV > 1.0`), dips to their cost basis trigger defense and buying. Once price closes decisively *below* STH Realized Price (`STH-MVRV < 0.97`), those same holders are trapped underwater and aggressively sell into rallies to exit at breakeven—turning STH Realized Price into a brick wall of resistance.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Microstructure Entry Verification (`CB-01`):** On-chain metrics update on daily/hourly block intervals and cannot pinpoint exact stop-loss placement. Always drop down to **Pillar 2 (15m/1H Volume Profile VAL/VAH + USD Spot CVD)** to execute entries at STH bands.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Entity-Adjusted / ETF Cohort Calibration (`CB-02`):** Always verify that STH cost-basis reclaims align with positive US Spot ETF netflows and wallet cohort accumulation (`100–10,000 BTC` sharks/whales).
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Gate:** In a macro liquidity contraction (`10Y yields > 5.0%`, `DXY > 103`, contracting stablecoins), `STH-MVRV < 1.0` signals prolonged bear-regime underwater stress rather than a quick V-bounce.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-13A: Bull Market Cost-Basis Defense ("STH-MVRV = 1.00 Support Rebound")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Primary macro regime is bullish (`Price > 200D MA`, Pillar 1 grants risk permission).
  2. Price pulls back into the **STH Realized Price band (`STH-MVRV = 0.98 to 1.02`)** following a local rally.
  3. **Order-Flow Absorption:** At the STH cost-basis zone, 15m/1H Volume Profile VAL holds, **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** turns sharply positive, and `MOD 03` confirms excess perp leverage has been flushed.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Recent bull-market entrants refuse to sell at a loss; instead, fresh spot buyers step in at aggregate breakeven to defend the structural uptrend.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-probability cyclical higher-low formation launching the next multi-week leg toward the `+1.0 SD` to `+1.5 SD` STH-MVRV band (`1.25–1.35`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon a confirmed **15m/1H VAH or Developing POC reclaim** directly above the STH Realized Price band.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop `2.5% to 3.5%` below STH Realized Price (beneath the 4H absorption swing low).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at prior swing high / `STH-MVRV = 1.20`; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H VAL or exit fully at the `STH-MVRV >= 1.32` overheated band.

### CSU-13B: Cost-Basis Loss & Underwater Trap ("STH-MVRV Breakdown Below 0.97")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Spot price breaks decisively below **STH Realized Price** (`STH-MVRV < 0.97` for `>= 3 consecutive daily closes`, e.g., losing `$75,600` and breaking below `$73,000`).
  2. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** and US Spot ETF netflows are negative, and rallies back toward STH Realized Price stall with negative spot delta (`breakeven selling`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The entire `< 155-day` buyer cohort is now underwater. Every bounce toward their cost basis meets automated limit-sell walls from trapped traders trying to get out even.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Multi-week corrective markdown or range compression beneath STH Realized Price until weak hands capitulate down toward the True Market Mean or `-1.5 SD` STH band (`STH-MVRV ~ 0.80–0.85`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (Zero Spot Swing Exposure).** Do not buy rallies into STH Realized Price from below. Wait either for a high-volume **Reclaim (`Mutation M1`)** or deep capitulation into **`CSU-13D`**.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

While `MVRV` (`MOD 13`) measures *unrealized* profit/loss sitting in wallets, **SOPR** measures *actual coins moving on-chain and locking in realized profit or loss*. Before acting on Module 14, four circuit breakers apply:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The 1.00 Equilibrium Polarity Rule (`CB-03`):**
  * **In a Bull Regime:** When `STH-SOPR` dips to `1.00`, holders refuse to sell at a loss, drying up spot supply and causing price to bounce immediately off `1.00`.
  * **In a Bear Regime:** When `STH-SOPR` has been below `1.00` for weeks, underwater holders wait anxiously to get back to breakeven. The moment price rallies enough to push `STH-SOPR` back up to `1.00`, they dump at breakeven—capping the rally.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Entity-Adjusted Filtering (`CB-02`):** Always use **Entity-Adjusted SOPR (`aSOPR`)** and `STH-SOPR` so internal exchange cold-to-hot wallet sweeps do not distort realized profit/loss ratios.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Capitulation Timing Filter (`CB-01`):** A deep `STH-SOPR` reading (`0.95`) tells you people are panicking *today*, not that the bottom is finished. Wait for `STH-SOPR` to stop making lower lows and confirm absorption on Pillar 2 order flow.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Permission Gate:** Verify secular liquidity and 200D MA status before trading a `1.00` SOPR reset bounce.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-14A: Bullish SOPR Reset ("1.00 Equilibrium Supply Exhaustion Bounce")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Primary macro trend is bullish (Pillar 1 grants risk permission; price above 200D MA).
  2. Following a multi-day pullback, **7D EMA `STH-SOPR` (and `aSOPR`) compresses down to `0.985–1.002`** (brief minor loss-taking shakeout) and **curls upward back above `1.00`**.
  3. **Order-Flow Confirmation:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** turns positive at 4H Volume Profile VAL, and `MOD 03` shows perp funding has normalized.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Weak short-term hands have finished flushing out at breakeven/slight loss; remaining holders refuse to sell below cost basis, creating an immediate on-chain supply vacuum.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-probability swing trend resumption upward as fresh spot demand meets depleted sell-side liquidity.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon a verified **15m/1H Volume Profile VAH or Developing POC reclaim** coinciding with `STH-SOPR` crossing back above `1.00`.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop beneath the 4H swing low where `STH-SOPR` bottomed.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% when `STH-SOPR` expands above `1.05–1.08` at overhead resistance; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H VAL or exit if `STH-SOPR` prints a bearish divergence (`price higher high, SOPR lower high`).

### CSU-14B: Bearish SOPR Rejection ("1.00 Breakeven Resistance Ceiling")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `STH-SOPR` has been depressed below `1.00` (`0.95–0.98`) for `>= 10–14 days` during a structural downtrend.
  2. Price stages a relief bounce, lifting **7D EMA `STH-SOPR` back up to `0.995–1.005`**, where it stalls and **turns back downward below `0.99`**.
  3. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** turns sharply negative at the bounce peak (`breakeven distribution`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Trapped short-term buyers used the relief rally to exit at breakeven, overwhelming intraday spot demand.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Rejection of the relief rally and resumption of the downward leg toward lower liquidity pools.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (Zero Spot Swing Exposure).** If holding any tactical bounce trade from lower levels, close 100% immediately as `STH-SOPR` hits `1.00` and stalls.

### CSU-14C: Profit-Taking Absorption ("Sustained High SOPR With Rising Price")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `aSOPR` and `LTH-SOPR` remain elevated (`1.05 to 1.15+`) for weeks as older coins realize substantial profits.
  2. **Absorption Proof:** Despite heavy on-chain profit realization, **price continues making higher highs**, **US Spot ETF inflows / Domestic USD Spot CVD** absorb every wave of profit-taking, and dips never break `STH-SOPR = 0.99`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Bull-market wealth transfer is healthy—new institutional capital is large enough to absorb Long-Term Holder profit-taking without breaking market structure.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Continued structural bull advance until either institutional inflows dry up (`Mutation M3`) or retail leverage overheats (`MOD 02`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **HOLD CORE SPOT SWING POSITION.** Continue trailing stops behind 4H Volume Profile VALs; do not prematurely exit a bull trend solely because `SOPR > 1.05` while price and USD Spot CVD keep climbing.

### CSU-14D: Capitulation Realized-Loss Climax ("STH-SOPR <= 0.94–0.96 Exhaustion")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Daily `STH-SOPR` plunges to **`<= 0.94–0.96`** (`<= 5th rolling percentile`), accompanied by a massive spike in net realized losses (`USD`).
  2. **Reversal Confirmation:** Within 24–72 hours, `STH-SOPR` prints a sharp **higher low** even as price retests or marginally sweeps the low, while **Domestic USD Spot CVD** turns positive and `MOD 03` confirms a full OI wipeout.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Panic sellers have dumped their coins at steep realized losses into institutional limit bids; marginal sellers at these prices are exhausted.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Authorize **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon 15m/1H **VAL or Developing POC reclaim** following the `STH-SOPR` higher-low divergence, sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling**.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Realized Capitalization values every Bitcoin UTXO at the price it last moved on-chain, making **30-Day Realized Cap Impulse** the purest measure of **net new fiat dollars entering or exiting the Bitcoin network**. Before acting on Module 15, four circuit breakers apply:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Net Capital Outflow Guard (`CB-01`):** When 30-day Realized Cap contracts (`net capital leaving the network at a realized loss`), Bitcoin is in a macro capital-drain regime. Tactical swing entries must be blocked or strictly gated until Realized Cap flattens and institutional cohorts (`100–10,000 BTC`) resume accumulation.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Custodian & ETF Wallet Separation (`CB-02`):** Modern exchange reserve tracking must filter out Coinbase Custody ETF creations/redemptions and internal exchange wallet migrations so synthetic balance shifts are not mistaken for organic spot withdrawals.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Absorption Deficit Check (`CB-03`):** Long-Term Holders always distribute into bull markets; LTH selling is only bearish when **LTH distribution exceeds fresh Realized Cap / Spot ETF inflows**, causing price to stall and roll over.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Sovereign Cold-Storage Alignment:** Aligns tactical swing risk (`0.75%` ceiling) with long-term sovereign custody allocation (`multi-vendor hardware multi-sig`).

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-15A: Fresh Capital Inflow & Healthy Wealth Transfer ("Cycle Expansion Engine")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **30-Day Realized Cap Impulse** expands strongly (`>= +2.0% to +5.0%+` per month), confirming massive net new USD capital entering Bitcoin.
  2. **LTH-to-STH Transfer:** Long-Term Holder supply declines gradually (`controlled distribution into strength`) while **US Spot ETF inflows**, **`100–10,000 BTC` entities**, and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** absorb every coin, driving price to higher highs.
  3. **Exchange Reserves:** Adjusted spot exchange BTC balances trend steadily downward into cold custody.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Textbook bull-market wealth transfer—veteran holders gradually realize gains into deep, persistent institutional and sovereign inflows without overwhelming order books.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained multi-week to multi-month bull trend where every derivative flush (`MOD 03`) is rapidly absorbed by fresh incoming capital.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Authorize full **0.75% Risk Ceiling** Unleveraged Spot Long entries on every Pillar 2/3 pullback setup (`MOD 01`, `MOD 03`, `MOD 13A`).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Bank 50% at upper Garman-Klass volatility bands / local resistance; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail generously beneath 4H/1D Volume Profile VALs until Realized Cap impulse decelerates (`Mutation M2`).

### CSU-15B: Unabsorbed LTH Distribution Top ("Cycle Exhaustion Ceiling")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **LTH Supply** drops sharply (`accelerated veteran distribution` at `>= 90th percentile` spending velocity) alongside `STH-MVRV >= 1.30` (`MOD 13C`).
  2. **30-Day Realized Cap Impulse** begins **flattening or decelerating** while **US Spot ETF inflows stall/flip negative**, and price prints a multi-week distribution range (failing to make new highs despite heavy volume).
  3. **`100–10,000 BTC` cohort balances** flip from net accumulation to net distribution.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Veteran supply has overwhelmed incoming institutional liquidity; late retail STH buyers are holding the bag at peak cost basis.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High probability of a major macro distribution breakdown (`-20% to -40%` cycle drawdown) once marginal STH demand exhausts.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **EXECUTE FULL TACTICAL EXIT (100% CASH ON SWING BOOK).** Shift sovereign focus strictly to capital preservation and multi-sig custody security until Realized Cap and cohort accumulation reset.

### CSU-15C: LTH Re-Accumulation & Shark Absorption ("Silent Structural Bottoming")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following a major `-30% to -45%` drawdown from cycle highs (benchmark: Nov 2026 `-41%` drawdown from `$126,080` into `$68K–$75K`), **30-Day Realized Cap stops falling** (`flattens near 0.0%`).
  2. **LTH Supply** climbs steadily (`coins aging past 155 days into strong hands`) and **`100–10,000 BTC` entities** show sustained net accumulation even while retail (`< 100 BTC`) and ETF headlines remain fearful (`Fear & Greed <= 25`).
  3. Price forms a multi-week base above a major HTF Volume Profile HVN with rising **Domestic USD Spot CVD**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 17 can authorize capital deployment, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The 200-Day Moving Average Secular Divider (`CB-01`):** Across Bitcoin's entire market history, trading above a rising 200D MA defines secular expansion (`full 0.75% risk permitted`), whereas trading below a declining 200D MA defines capital-preservation defense (`maximum 0.375% risk only on extreme capitulation resets, otherwise 100% Cash`).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — The New York Session Truth Test (`CB-02`):** In the Spot ETF era, **New York hours (13:30–21:00 UTC)** dictate structural direction. Rallies built on offshore leverage during Asia hours routinely get faded by NY ETF/institutional desks if US flows are negative. Always wait for the NY cash open to validate overnight moves.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Passive Index & Forced Corporate Flow Stand-Down (`CB-03`):** When major equity/index providers (such as MSCI removals of Bitcoin treasury proxies) or quarter-end institutional rebalancings execute forced mechanical selling, order flow is price-insensitive. Stand down in **100% Cash** until the mechanical rebalancing window closes.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Weekend / CME Gap Discipline:** Avoid initiating swing positions during thin Saturday–Sunday offshore chop; wait for Sunday 22:00 UTC CME futures open and Monday NY liquidity.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-17A: Secular 200D MA Reclaim & Retest ("Macro Bull Regime Ignition")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Bitcoin reclaims its **200-Day Moving Average** from below with a strong volume-backed impulse (`MOD 08A`) and holds above the 200D MA for `>= 3 consecutive daily closes`.
  2. **Retest Confirmation:** Price pulls back to kiss the **200D MA from above**, where **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** and US Spot ETF netflows surge positive (`absorbing all sellers`) and 1H/4H Volume Profile VAL holds.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Secular trend-following funds, CTA algorithms, and institutional asset allocators flip from net-short/neutral back to systematic long allocation.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Multi-month structural uptrend with high-probability higher lows along the rising 200D MA.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon the confirmed **15m/1H VAH reclaim off the 200D MA retest**, sized to the full **0.75% Risk Ceiling** with hard structural stop `3%–4%` below the 200D MA swing low.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at `+3.0R` or `STH-MVRV = 1.25`; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H/1D Volume Profile VALs while price holds above the 200D MA.

### CSU-17B: Asia/London Stop Sweep -> New York Institutional Reversal ("NY Absorption Session")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. During **Asia (00:00–08:00 UTC)** or **London (08:00–13:30 UTC)** hours, price sweeps downward below the prior day's low / 4H VAL, triggering offshore long liquidations (`MOD 03`).
  2. **New York Session Reversal (13:30–16:30 UTC):** Immediately upon the US equity/ETF cash open, **Coinbase USD Spot CVD surges vertically positive**, price reclaims the London/Asia range VAL, and `A_CVD,OI` flips positive.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** US institutional desks used overnight offshore liquidity sweeps to accumulate discounted spot inventory at the NY open.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Strong NY-session trend reversal reclaiming the entire overnight drop and squeezing late European/Asian breakdown shorts.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon the **15m/1H close back above the overnight VAL** between `13:30 and 16:30 UTC`, sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling** (`0.375%` if below 200D MA) with stop beneath the overnight sweep low.

### CSU-17C: Asia Leveraged Perp Pump -> New York Spot Fade ("The Offshore Pump Trap")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. During **Asia hours (00:00–08:00 UTC)**, Bitcoin rallies `+2% to +5%` driven by rising offshore Open Interest (`Binance/OKX/Bybit`) and positive funding, while **Domestic USD Spot CVD is flat**.
  2. **NY Cash Open Rejection (13:30–15:30 UTC):** At the US market open, **Coinbase USD Spot CVD turns steeply negative**, price loses the Asia session POC/VAL, and `A_P,V` rolls over negative.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Offshore retail leverage chased an illiquid overnight bounce directly into waiting US institutional spot/ETF sell programs.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Complete retracement of the Asia pump plus a downward stop-hunt through the prior day's low.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL MORNING LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** If holding a tactical position from the prior day, exit immediately upon loss of the Asia POC at the NY open.

### CSU-17D: Forced Index Rebalancing / Quarterly OpEx Stand-Down ("Mechanical Flow Freeze")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Market is inside a known **forced mechanical flow window**: passive index rebalancing effective date (e.g., MSCI/S&P constituent adjustments), quarter-end institutional rebalancing (`final 48 hours of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec`), or quarterly Deribit/CME Options Expiration (`final 24 hours before 08:00 UTC Friday settlement`).
  2. Order flow shows large, mechanical TWAP selling or extreme pin gravity around Max Pain strikes regardless of intraday sentiment.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — USDT De-Peg Adjustment (`CB-02`):** Because Coinbase quotes in true US Dollars (`BTC-USD`) and Binance/Bybit quote in Tether (`BTC-USDT`), any stablecoin peg deviation distorts raw spreads. Always normalize `CPI_adjusted = (Coinbase_USD - (Binance_USDT * USDT_USD)) / Coinbase_USD`.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Offshore Pump Veto (`CB-01`):** Whenever Bitcoin rallies on offshore perps while `CPI_pct <= -0.10%` (`Z <= -1.25`), US spot desks and ETF Authorized Participants are using offshore liquidity to sell. All long breakout signals are vetoed.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — NY Session Weighting (`CB-04`):** Coinbase Premium carries maximum predictive weight during **US institutional hours (13:30–21:00 UTC)**. Overnight weekend or Asia-hour spread noise must be confirmed at the NY open.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Permission Gate:** Even a sharp positive spike in Coinbase Premium must pass Pillar 1 (`MOD 16`) before deploying full `0.75%` risk.

**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** November 2026 Regulated Derivatives Telemetry — CME Bitcoin Futures trading at a regulated institutional contango basis of **+5.3% to +5.7% annualized** even while offshore perpetuals dipped into negative basis (`-0.047%`), alongside CME Open Interest contracting **~15%** during the November 10–13 institutional de-risking from **$75,771** down to **$68,450** prior to the Friday `21:00 UTC` CME weekly close.

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Weekend Thin-Liquidity Trap (`CB-01`):** Because CME Group closes Friday at `21:00 UTC` and reopens Sunday at `22:00 UTC`, weekend price moves occur on thin offshore order books without institutional arbitrageurs. Weekend breakouts fail over **70% of the time** once CME reopens.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — CME Basis vs. Offshore Perp Divergence (`CB-02`):** CME futures are dominated by regulated US hedge funds, asset managers, and ETF market makers. When CME annualized basis compresses toward money-market T-bill rates (`~4%–5%`) while CME OI falls (`-15%`), institutions are unwinding long exposure regardless of offshore retail excitement.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Monthly Contract Roll Distortion (`CB-03`):** During the final 48 hours before CME monthly expiry (last Friday of each month at `16:00 UTC`), OI shifts between front-month and next-month contracts; do not mistake roll volume for directional positioning.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Gap-Fill Confirmation Rule (`CB-04`):** Never blindly fade a weekend gap without Pillar 2 confirmation (`15m/1H VAH/VAL rejection or reclaim + Domestic USD Spot CVD`).

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-20A: Downward Weekend Gap Fill Reversal ("Sunday Sweep & Reclaim Magnet")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Over Saturday–Sunday, Bitcoin drops **`>= 1.5% to 4.0%` below Friday's `21:00 UTC` CME Close** on thin offshore volume, leaving an open downside CME gap above spot.
  2. **Sunday Open / Monday NY Absorption:** Following Sunday `22:00 UTC` CME reopen (or Monday `13:30 UTC` NY open), price sweeps an intraday liquidity low, **reclaims the 15m/1H Volume Profile VAL**, and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** + Coinbase Premium (`MOD 19`) turn positive.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Offshore weekend sellers pushed price away from institutional fair value while CME desks were offline; upon reopen, institutional arbitrageurs and spot buyers step in to close the gap.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-probability upward rotation back to **100% fill of Friday's `21:00 UTC` CME Close price**.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon confirmed **15m/1H VAL reclaim** after Sunday `22:00 UTC` with positive Domestic USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop beneath the weekend / Sunday-open sweep wick low.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (70% of Position):** Close 70% directly at the **exact Friday `21:00 UTC` CME Gap Fill level**; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 30%):** Trail behind 1H VAL if Domestic USD Spot CVD continues expanding through the gap.

### CSU-20B: Upward Weekend Gap Bull Trap ("Illiquid Sunday Pump Fade")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Over Saturday–Sunday, Bitcoin rallies **`>= 1.5% to 4.0%` above Friday's `21:00 UTC` CME Close** on rising offshore perp OI (`Binance/Bybit`) and thin weekend spot volume (`< 30th percentile`).
  2. At Sunday `22:00 UTC` CME reopen or Monday `13:30 UTC` NY open, **Coinbase Premium (`MOD 19`) is negative (`<= -0.08%`)** and price loses the Sunday-open 1H POC/VAL.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Offshore retail traders chased an illiquid weekend pump, creating a high-gravity CME gap below spot that US institutional sellers immediately target at the open.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Downward mean-reversion flush filling 100% of the upward weekend gap back to Friday's CME close.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL SUNDAY/MONDAY MORNING LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** If holding an existing Spot Long from Friday, bank 50%–100% profit into the upward weekend gap ahead of the Monday NY open.

### CSU-20C: CME Institutional Basis Expansion ("Regulated Leverage Tailwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. CME front-month annualized basis expands from `5%` above **`+10.0% to +16.0%` APR** alongside **rising CME Open Interest** and **positive US Spot ETF net inflows**.
  2. Perpetual basis (`MOD 02`) remains disciplined (`< +0.025%`), showing regulated institutions—not reckless retail—are driving the futures premium.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Regulated hedge funds and institutional allocators are adding directional long exposure via CME futures while ETF demand absorbs underlying spot supply.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained multi-week institutional bull trend.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **AUTHORIZE FULL `0.75% RISK` SPOT SWING ENTRIES** on Pillar 2 pullbacks (`MOD 10B / 11A`).

### CSU-20D: CME Institutional De-Risking Unwind ("Basis Compression & OI Drain")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. CME Open Interest contracts sharply (`>= -10% to -15%` over 5–10 sessions) while CME annualized basis compresses toward or below the **US 3-Month T-Bill risk-free rate (`<= +4.5% APR`)** alongside US Spot ETF outflows.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Regulated institutions are closing long futures and unwinding basis trades—removing the primary institutional bid pillar from the market.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Heavy overhead supply and downside vulnerability until CME OI stabilizes.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE 100% CASH LOCK** until CME OI stops contracting and Domestic USD Spot CVD turns positive.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The L2 Order-Book Mirage Law (`CB-01`):** Public order-book heatmaps show *intent*, not *commitment*. Spoofing algorithms routinely flash $20M–$50M bid walls below spot to trick retail traders into buying, then cancel the wall milliseconds before price touches it. **Never trust a resting wall until actual executed trades (tape/footprint) prove the wall absorbed volume without pulling.**
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Iceberg vs. Spoof Distinction:**
  * **Spoof Wall:** Visible size = 500 BTC $\rightarrow$ Price approaches $\rightarrow$ Order canceled $\rightarrow$ Executed volume = 5 BTC. *(Trap — Do Not Trade).*
  * **Institutional Iceberg:** Visible size = 25 BTC $\rightarrow$ Market orders hit it repeatedly $\rightarrow$ Executed volume = 600 BTC at the exact same price level without breaking *(True Absorption — Trade With It)*.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Respect Active Institutional TWAP Sellers (`CB-03`):** When an Authorized Participant (AP) or large fund executes a 4-hour TWAP sell program to settle ETF redemptions, trying to catch the bottom mid-program bleeds capital. Wait for the TWAP cadence to stop and Session VWAP to be reclaimed.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Domestic Venue Verification (`CB-02`):** True institutional TWAP algorithms execute on regulated USD venues (Coinbase Prime, Kraken, Bitstamp, LMAX) to minimize market impact.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-21A: Verified Iceberg Bid Absorption + TWAP Buy Ignition ("Institutional Floor & Lift")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. At a key structural level (Volume Profile VAL, 4H HVN, or STH Realized Price), the 15m footprint records **executed sell volume exceeding visible L2 bid depth by `>= 5x` at a single price band without price breaking lower** (`verified passive iceberg absorption`).
  2. Immediately following absorption, **Coinbase/Kraken USD Spot CVD** begins climbing in a **steady, linear staircase (`TWAP buy algorithm`)**, reclaiming **Session VWAP** and the 15m Developing POC.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** A large institutional buyer first absorbed all panicked market sellers via a hidden iceberg bid, and has now switched to an automated TWAP accumulation algorithm to build a multi-hour/multi-day spot position.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Steady, relentless upward price grind supported by continuous algorithmic bid replenishment on every minor dip to Session VWAP.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on a pullback touch of **Session VWAP / 15m Developing POC** while the linear USD Spot CVD staircase remains active.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop immediately beneath the **verified iceberg absorption price floor**.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at the upper `2.0-Sigma VWAP Standard Deviation Band` / overhead VAH; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind Session/Anchored VWAP until the TWAP buy staircase flattens and price closes below VWAP.

### CSU-21B: Institutional TWAP Distribution Suppression ("VWAP Ceiling Pin")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Domestic USD Spot CVD** declines in a smooth, relentless downward staircase (`algorithmic TWAP sell program`), capping every intraday bounce directly at **Session VWAP** or the 1H POC.
  2. Every time price approaches Session VWAP, hidden iceberg ask orders absorb market buys and push price back down (`negative delta at VWAP`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional desks or ETF redemption programs are systematically liquidating spot inventory in time-sliced blocks to avoid crashing the book all at once, patiently selling into every retail bounce.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Grinding downward stair-step breakdown until the TWAP sell quota is completely filled.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (Zero Dip-Buying Permitted).** Do not buy any bounce while price remains trapped below a declining Session VWAP with linear negative USD Spot CVD. Wait for `Mutation M1` (TWAP exhaustion + VWAP reclaim).

### CSU-21C: Bid-Wall Spoof Rug-Pull Trap ("Liquidity Mirage Breakdown")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Large resting bid walls appear on L2 order books `0.5%–1.5%` below spot (`creating bullish heatmap optics`), attracting retail front-running buyers (`Perp CVD spikes up`).
  2. As spot price drifts down toward the wall, **the resting bid wall is canceled (`pulled`) before absorbing significant volume**, and price slices cleanly through the level on thin volume (`MOD 08D`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market makers or large sellers flashed fake support to induce retail longs to buy higher up, then pulled the bid so they could fill lower or trigger stop-losses.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sharp air-pocket drop through the vacated price zone.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **CIRCUIT BREAKER 1 (`CB-01`) ENFORCED: DO NOT ENTER.** If already long, exit immediately if price breaks through the pulled-wall price level.

### CSU-21D: Ask-Wall Spoof & Stop-Hunt Breakout ("Upward Liquidity Sweep")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Large resting ask walls sit just above resistance (`VAH`), suppressing price while **Domestic USD Spot CVD quietly accumulates below the wall**.
  2. Suddenly, the ask wall is **pulled** (or eaten in a single institutional sweep) and price explodes through VAH on surge volume (`MOD 11B`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Accumulating institutions used a resting ask wall to cap price while filling their TWAP orders below resistance; once filled, they removed the cap and let price reprice higher.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on the first 15m pullback retest of the **cleared ask-wall / VAH level** (now acting as support), sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling**.

**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** November 2026 Federal Reserve & US Treasury Plumbing Telemetry — US Treasury General Account (**TGA**) elevated at **$912.13B** (draining private bank reserves), Overnight Reverse Repo (**RRP**) facility near exhaustion, US 10-Year Yield at **5.129%**, and crypto stablecoins contracting **-$14.2B in 30 days**—confirming an active **Net Dollar Liquidity Drain (`GATE_RED`)**.

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Why **Net Fed Liquidity (`WALCL − TGA − RRP`)** is the master plumbing gauge of global risk assets:
* When the US Treasury refills its checking account at the Fed (**TGA rises**), it pulls cash directly out of commercial bank reserves and money markets.
* Previously, rising TGA was cushioned by money-market funds draining the **Reverse Repo (RRP)** facility. **Once RRP approaches zero (`< $100B`), every dollar added to TGA drains commercial bank reserves 1-for-1**, starving risk assets of dollar liquidity.
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The RRP Exhaustion Multiplier (`CB-01`):** Whenever `RRP < $100B` AND `TGA > $850B` (benchmark: **$912.13B** in Nov 2026), any further Treasury debt issuance drains bank liquidity immediately—forcing Pillar 1 into heightened defensive posture (`GATE_AMBER` or `GATE_RED`).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Tax-Date TGA Surge Protection (`CB-03`):** Around major US tax collection dates, TGA jumps sharply over 10–15 days, routinely triggering equity and Bitcoin pullbacks.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Lead-Lag Calibration (`CB-04`):** Liquidity plumbing shifts lead Bitcoin spot price by **2 to 6 weeks**; combine `MOD 22` with `MOD 16` (10Y Yield, DXY, Stablecoins) to set the active Pillar 1 Gate.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-22A: Treasury TGA Drain & Reserve Injection ("Stealth QE Liquidity Tailwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Net Fed Liquidity (`WALCL − TGA − RRP`)** expands by **`>= +$75B` over 4 weeks** and **`>= +$150B` over 13 weeks** (driven by Treasury spending down the TGA below `$700B`, Fed balance-sheet expansion, or RRP outflows).
  2. Confirmed by `MOD 16` (`DXY falling`, `30D Stablecoin supply expanding >= +1.5%`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Dollars are flowing out of the Treasury's Fed account directly into private commercial bank reserves and institutional balance sheets, lowering funding stress and fueling asset inflation.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Strong structural macro tailwind supporting multi-week Bitcoin trend expansion and rapid absorption of derivatives dips.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`REINFORCE PILLAR 1 = GATE_GREEN_FULL_RISK` (`0.75% Risk Ceiling`).**
  * **Action:** Authorize full-size unleveraged spot swing entries on all validated Pillar 2/3 setups and maximize `Stage 2` trailing runners.

### CSU-22B: TGA Refill Drain With Depleted RRP ("Bank Reserve Vacuum")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **TGA surges above `$850B–$900B+`** (benchmark: **$912.13B** in Nov 2026) due to heavy net Treasury bill/bond issuance or tax receipts, while **RRP sits below `$100B`** (zero buffer left).
  2. **4-Week Net Fed Liquidity contracts by `<= -$75B to -$150B`**, accompanied by rising SOFR/repo rates, rising 10Y yields (`> 5.0%`), and contracting crypto stablecoins (`MOD 16C`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The US Treasury is vacuuming dollar liquidity out of private markets to fund fiscal deficits without an RRP cushion, tightening dollar availability globally.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High risk of liquidity air pockets, thin crypto order books (`$72M depth`), and failed breakout rallies until TGA peaks and begins spending down.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`DOWNGRADE PILLAR 1 TO GATE_RED_100_CASH_LOCK` (or strict `GATE_AMBER` if 200D MA holds).**
  * **Action:** Lock **100% Cash** on tactical swing capital; preserve dry powder until `Mutation M1` signals TGA spend-down inflection.

### CSU-22C: Neutral Plumbing / Offset Flows ("Balanced Reserve Regime")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `4-Week Net Fed Liquidity` remains within **`-$40B to +$40B`** (e.g., modest Fed QT offset by steady TGA spending).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Macro plumbing is neutral; Bitcoin price action will be governed by crypto-native flows (`Spot ETFs`, `Stablecoins`, `Perp OI/CVD`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Defer gate authority to `MOD 16` (Stablecoins/DXY/Yields) and `MOD 17` (200D MA).

### CSU-22D: Emergency Fed Liquidity Facility / Balance Sheet Expansion ("Crisis-to-Bull Pivot")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following a banking, bond-market (`MOVE > 130`), or sovereign funding stress event, the Federal Reserve halts QT, cuts rates, or launches an emergency lending facility—causing **`WALCL` (Fed Total Assets) to jump `>= +$100B` in 1–2 weeks**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Explicit monetary debasement and central-bank liquidity backstop—the single most powerful multi-month catalyst in Bitcoin's history.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Initial knee-jerk volatility followed by an explosive secular repricing higher in scarce monetary assets.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Once initial 24H–48H bond volatility settles (`MOD 23 M1`), **LOCK `GATE_GREEN_FULL_RISK`** and deploy both `0.75% Risk` Spot Swings and Sovereign Cold-Storage tranches (`MOD 18D`).

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Why **MOVE Index** and **High-Yield (Junk) Credit Spreads** act as Bitcoin's systemic early-warning system:
* US Treasuries are the foundational collateral of the entire global financial system. When **MOVE** (bond volatility) spikes, prime brokers and clearinghouses mechanically increase margin haircuts on Treasury collateral—forcing multi-strategy hedge funds to raise cash immediately by selling their most liquid, 24/7-traded asset: **Bitcoin**.
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Collateral Haircut Rule (`CB-03`):** Whenever `MOVE >= 120` (or surges `>= +20%` in 5 days), Bitcoin ceases trading on crypto fundamentals and trades strictly as an institutional ATM for cash raising. **100% Cash Lock is mandatory.**
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Credit Spread Confirmation (`CB-01`):** Rising 10Y yields driven by *healthy economic growth* (`HY credit spreads tight/falling`) are manageable for Bitcoin; rising yields or falling yields driven by *credit stress* (`HY OAS widening >= +40 bps`) trigger systemic deleveraging.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Volatility Peak Inflection:** The exact bottom of a cross-asset liquidation wave almost always coincides with the **intraday peak of the MOVE Index**—not before.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-23A: Calm Bond Volatility & Tight Credit Spreads ("Clean Collateral Tailwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **ICE BofA MOVE Index** sits below **`85`** (or trends steadily lower from elevated levels).
  2. **US High-Yield Credit Spreads (HY OAS)** are stable or compressing (`HYG` trading above its 50D/200D MA).
  3. Supported by `MOD 16` (`DXY stable/falling`, `Stablecoins expanding`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Predictable bond pricing lowers collateral haircuts across prime brokers, encouraging institutional risk-taking and steady inflows into Bitcoin spot and ETFs.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Low cross-asset correlation shocks; Bitcoin trades cleanly according to its internal Pillar 2, 3, and 5 supply/demand mechanics.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`PERMIT FULL 0.75% RISK CEILING`** across validated Pillar 2/3 setups.

### CSU-23B: Elevated Rate Volatility Regime ("MOVE 90–115 Caution Zone")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **MOVE Index** rises into the **`90 to 115` zone** (benchmark: **92.44** on Nov 12–13, 2026) as Treasury yields reprice rapidly around inflation/Fed uncertainty, while HY credit spreads remain contained (`< +30 bps` widening).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional desks reduce gross leverage and widen stop buffers ahead of rate volatility, thinning out Bitcoin order-book depth (`$72M depth`).
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Choppy, headline-sensitive price action with sharp intraday wicks around US bond market hours (`12:00–20:00 UTC`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`CAP PILLAR 1 AT GATE_AMBER (Max 0.375% Risk)`** (or `GATE_RED` if combined with `MOD 16C` stablecoin drain). Require wider Garman-Klass stop calibration (`MOD 12`).

### CSU-23C: Systemic Bond & Credit Stress Shock ("MOVE >= 120 / Credit Blowout Cash Lock")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **MOVE Index spikes above `120–130+`** (or jumps `>= +20%` within 5 sessions), **OR** **High-Yield Credit Spreads (HY OAS)** widen by `>= +40 to +75 bps` in 10 sessions (`HYG` breaking down sharply).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Active systemic liquidity crunch—institutions are facing margin calls across bond and credit books and liquidating Bitcoin spot/ETFs indiscriminately for USD cash.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Violent multi-day correlation-1.0 sell-off across equities, credit, and crypto.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`EMERGENCY PILLAR 1 = GATE_RED_100_CASH_LOCK`.**
  * **Action:** Close 100% of active swing positions immediately; block all dip-buying until `Mutation M1` confirms MOVE and credit spreads have peaked.

### CSU-23D: Post-Crisis Bond Volatility Crush ("MOVE Peak Reversal Entry")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. After spiking above `120+`, the **MOVE Index prints a sharp reversal downward (`dropping >= -15% off peak` over 3–5 sessions)** as Fed intervention or dovish policy calms Treasury markets.
  2. Bitcoin holds a higher-timeframe support floor (`MOD 03A / 13D`) and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** turns positive.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Collateral haircuts relax and forced institutional liquidation stops abruptly—releasing pent-up spot demand into an oversold Bitcoin market.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Upgrade Pillar 1 from `GATE_RED` to `GATE_AMBER` (`0.375% Risk`), authorizing Spot Long entry upon 15m/1H **VAH/VAL reclaim** confirmed by USD Spot CVD.

```yaml
  CB_02_VENUE_CONTAMINATION: "Monitor spot USD/JPY and 1-month USD/JPY implied volatility across Tokyo (00:00-06:00 UTC) and London/NY sessions."
  CB_03_FORCED_FLOW_STANDDOWN: "Enforce automatic 24H-48H Cash Stand-Down during Bank of Japan (BOJ) policy meetings and suspected MOF FX intervention spikes."
```

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Why **USD/JPY** is one of the most dangerous hidden liquidation triggers for Bitcoin:
* Global macro funds borrow cheap Japanese Yen (`JPY`) to fund leveraged long positions in US tech equities and Bitcoin (`the Yen Carry Trade`).
* When the Bank of Japan hikes rates, yields surge, or Japan's Ministry of Finance intervenes to defend the Yen near **160.00**, **USD/JPY plunges violently** (Yen strengthens rapidly). Every 1% jump in the Yen instantly inflates the debt burden of Yen-funded carry books—forcing macro funds to dump liquid 24/7 Bitcoin holdings to meet margin calls.
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Violent Yen-Spike Veto (`CB-01`):** Whenever `USD/JPY` drops `>= -1.5%` in 24 hours (or `>= -3.0%` in 5 days), **lock 100% Cash immediately**. Do not try to catch Bitcoin support until `USD/JPY` stabilizes.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — The 160.00 Intervention Ceiling (`CB-03`):** When `USD/JPY` trades above **158.00–160.00+** (benchmark: **159.88–160.13** on Nov 13, 2026), bilateral FX intervention can strike without warning during Tokyo or NY hours, causing a 300–500 pip flash crash in `USD/JPY` and an immediate sympathy flush in BTC.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — BOJ Policy Meeting Freeze (`CB-03`):** Stand down from new swing entries within `+/- 12 hours` of Bank of Japan rate decisions.

### CSU-24A: Benign Carry Equilibrium ("Stable FX Liquidity Environment")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `USD/JPY` trades smoothly below intervention danger thresholds (`< 155.00`) with 5-day realized FX range `< 1.5%`.
  2. US-Japan yield differentials are stable and `MOVE Index < 85` (`MOD 23A`).

  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Cross-border carry funding is stable; global macro funds face zero forced FX margin calls, leaving Bitcoin free to respond to spot ETF and on-chain inflows.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Normal cross-asset risk regime permitting full Pillar 2/3 swing execution.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

### CSU-24B: The 158–160+ Intervention Danger Zone ("Asymmetric FX Tail-Risk Ceiling")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `USD/JPY` climbs into or above **`158.00–160.00+`** (benchmark: **159.88–160.13** on Nov 13, 2026) driven by wide US-Japan yield spreads (`10Y > 5.0%`), triggering verbal warnings from Japanese Ministry of Finance officials.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market is coiled on a hair-trigger: either hot US yields push `USD/JPY` higher (draining global liquidity via strong USD) OR Japanese authorities intervene to smash `USD/JPY` lower (triggering an abrupt carry-trade margin unwind). Both outcomes create sharp asymmetric downside risk for Bitcoin.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High risk of sudden `200–500 pip` `USD/JPY` air pockets that trigger simultaneous crypto liquidation cascades.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`CAP PILLAR 1 AT GATE_AMBER (Max 0.375% Risk)`** (or `GATE_RED` if `MOD 16` stablecoins are draining). Tighten stops on any open swing positions ahead of Tokyo (`00:00–06:00 UTC`) and NY (`13:30–16:00 UTC`) intervention windows.

### CSU-24C: Active Yen Carry-Trade Unwind ("Cross-Asset Margin Liquidation Cascade")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `USD/JPY` plunges **`>= -1.5%` within 24 hours** (or `>= -3.0%` over 5 trading days) on surging volume, accompanied by falling Nasdaq/Nikkei futures and rising `MOVE` volatility.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Forced cross-asset deleveraging—macro funds are liquidating Bitcoin, gold, and tech equities simultaneously to pay back appreciating Yen loans.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Multi-day liquidation cascade that ignores local crypto support levels until `USD/JPY` stops falling.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Gate Output:** **`EMERGENCY PILLAR 1 = GATE_RED_100_CASH_LOCK`.**
  * **Action:** Exit any open tactical BTC exposure immediately; hold 100% Cash until `Mutation M1` confirms `USD/JPY` stabilization.

### CSU-24D: Post-Unwind FX Stabilization & Sovereign Rotation ("Carry Reset Rebound")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following a `-3%+` Yen carry unwind flush, `USD/JPY` forms a multi-day base (`24H range < 0.8%`), US 10Y yields stabilize/fall (`DXY < 102`), and Bitcoin reclaims 1H/4H Volume Profile **VAL** with positive **Domestic USD Spot CVD**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Forced Yen-funded carry sellers have finished liquidating; discounted Bitcoin is absorbed by unleveraged spot buyers.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Upgrade Pillar 1 to `GATE_AMBER` (`0.375% Risk`) and authorize Spot Long entry upon 15m/1H **VAH/VAL reclaim** (`MOD 11A`).

# [29/44] MOD 25 — Dealer Gamma Exposure (GEX)   Zero-Gamma Flip Mechanic

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 25: DEALER GAMMA EXPOSURE (GEX) & ZERO-GAMMA FLIP MECHANICS
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 & Pillar 3 Bridge (Options Market-Maker Delta-Hedging Flows & Volatility Amplification) cross-verified with Pillar 4 (Portfolio Engineering).
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** November 10–13, 2026 Deribit & Multi-Venue Options Telemetry — Aggregate BTC options Open Interest standing at **434,345 BTC ($32.85B)** across Deribit (`78.9%`), OKX (`10.2%`), Binance (`5.0%`), Bybit (`4.4%`), and Delta (`1.4%`), with the **Zero-Gamma Flip Level anchored at $75,000**, **Call Wall at $90,000**, and **Put Wall at $70,000**—so once spot broke below **$75,000**, dealers flipped into **Negative Gamma** and were mechanically forced to sell into the drop toward **$68,450**.

---

```yaml
# PRIMARY TELEMETRY TRIGGERS (DEALER DELTA-HEDGING REGIMES)
base_regime_triggers:
  zero_gamma_flip_level: "Strike price where aggregate Net Dealer GEX crosses 0.0 (benchmark $75,000 in Nov 2026)"
  positive_gamma_regime: "Spot_Price > Zero_Gamma_Flip AND Net_GEX > 0 (Dealers buy dips, sell rallies -> volatility suppression)"
  negative_gamma_regime: "Spot_Price < Zero_Gamma_Flip AND Net_GEX < 0 (Dealers sell dips, buy rallies -> momentum amplification)"
  major_gamma_walls: "Call Wall (largest positive GEX overhead strike; benchmark $90K) and Put Wall (largest negative GEX support strike; benchmark $70K)"

# ANTI-TRAP CIRCUIT BREAKERS
circuit_breakers:
  CB_01_PREMATURE_CLOSE: "NEVER buy a falling knife in a Negative Gamma regime (Spot < Zero-Gamma Flip) until a major Put Wall is swept and reclaimed with Domestic USD Spot CVD."
  CB_02_VENUE_CONTAMINATION: "Compute Net GEX across Deribit (core ~80% liquidity) + OKX/Binance/Bybit composite option chains."
  CB_03_FORCED_FLOW_STANDDOWN: "When Spot breaks cleanly below the major Put Wall (e.g., losing $70K Put Wall), dealers must aggressively short futures to hedge—stand down 100% in Cash."
  CB_04_SIGNAL_HALF_LIFE: "GEX walls reset sharply after weekly/monthly Friday 08:00 UTC expiries; always recompute the Zero-Gamma Flip immediately post-expiry."

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)

  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
  exit_protocol: "TWO_STAGE_STRUCTURAL"
```

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Why **Dealer Gamma Exposure (GEX)** governs whether breakouts stick or mean-revert:
* Options market makers (dealers) remain delta-neutral by dynamically hedging their book in spot and perpetual futures:
  * **Above the Zero-Gamma Flip (`Positive Gamma`):** Dealers are net long gamma—when price rises, they **sell** futures; when price drops, they **buy** futures. This dampens volatility and pins price inside ranges.
  * **Below the Zero-Gamma Flip (`Negative Gamma`):** Dealers are net short gamma—when price drops, they are mechanically forced to **sell** futures into the hole; when price rises, they are forced to **buy** into the rally. This creates violent trend acceleration and wide ranges.
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Put-Wall Breach Stand-Down (`CB-03`):** The **Put Wall** (strike with largest put OI/negative gamma, e.g., `$70,000`) acts as support *only* while spot stays above it. Once price breaks decisively *below* the Put Wall, dealer delta-hedging flips into forced selling, accelerating the drop toward the next lower strike cluster.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Negative Gamma Stop Calibration (`CB-04`):** In a Negative Gamma regime, intraday swings expand by `1.5x–2.0x`. Always widen your structural stop distance accordingly and shrink BTC position size so your dollar loss never exceeds `0.75%`.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-25A: Zero-Gamma Flip Reclaim ("Transition From Acceleration to Institutional Pinning")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Bitcoin reclaims the **Zero-Gamma Flip Level** from below (benchmark: reclaiming **$75,000**) with a confirmed 1H/4H Volume Profile **VAH** close.
  2. **Order-Flow Confirmation:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** (`MOD 05/19`) is positive, and dealer short-gamma covering forces market makers to buy futures as price crosses the flip point.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market structure transitions from unstable downward acceleration into dealer-stabilized upward drift toward the overhead **Call Wall** (`$80K–$90K`).
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sharp initial squeeze through the Zero-Gamma Flip followed by orderly, supported trend progression.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on a 15m/1H pullback retest of the reclaimed **Zero-Gamma Flip Level** with positive Domestic USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% halfway to or at the first major **Positive Gamma Peak / Call Wall** (where dealer selling dampens upside); move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H VAL or close fully at the primary **Call Wall**.

### CSU-25B: Put-Wall Sweep & Reclaim in Negative Gamma ("Dealer Short-Cover Reversal")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. In a Negative Gamma regime (`Spot < Zero-Gamma Flip`), price sweeps downward through the major **Put Wall** (e.g., sweeping below `$70,000` toward `$68,000`), triggering peak dealer hedging sales and perp liquidations (`MOD 03A`).
  2. **Reclaim Confirmation:** Price snaps back **above the Put Wall** on the 15m/1H chart, **Domestic USD Spot CVD** surges positive (`absorbing the dealer hedge flow`), and 1H VAL is reclaimed.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Because dealers are short gamma below the flip, once spot reclaims the Put Wall, those exact same dealers are mechanically forced to **buy back** the futures they just sold—fueling a rapid squeeze back toward the Zero-Gamma Flip.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Fast V-shaped mean-reversion rally from the Put Wall back toward the Zero-Gamma Flip level.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon confirmed **15m/1H close back above the Put Wall + VAL** backed by positive USD Spot CVD, sized strictly to `0.75%` (`0.375%` if Pillar 1 is `GATE_AMBER`).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (60% of Position):** Close 60% just below the **Zero-Gamma Flip Level** (where dealer buying pressure neutralizes); move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 40%):** Hold only if price breaks cleanly through the Zero-Gamma Flip (`CSU-25A`).

### CSU-25C: Negative Gamma Breakdown Below Put Wall ("Dealer Hedging Waterfall")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Spot trades below the **Zero-Gamma Flip** (`Net GEX < 0`) AND breaks cleanly **below the major Put Wall** (`1H close below Put Wall`) with **Domestic USD Spot CVD negative**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market makers are forced to sell progressively larger amounts of spot/futures as price falls further into negative gamma territory, creating an air-pocket cascade toward the next lower open-interest strike.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Rapid downward acceleration with failed minor bounces.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE STRICT 100% CASH LOCK.** Stand down completely until price reaches the next major lower strike cluster and satisfies `CSU-25B` reclaim rules.

### CSU-25D: Positive Gamma Call-Wall Pinning ("Low-Volatility Mean-Reversion Chop")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Spot trades cleanly above the **Zero-Gamma Flip (`Net GEX >> 0`)** and approaches a massive overhead **Call Wall** (e.g., `$90,000` with `$20M+` gamma density), while Garman-Klass volatility compresses (`MOD 12`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Dealers sell into every uptick near the Call Wall and buy every dip toward the Zero-Gamma Flip, pinning price in a tight range.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BANK STAGE 2 PROFITS AT THE CALL WALL.** Do not chase breakouts directly into a massive Call Wall until after OpEx expiry clears the gamma concentration (`MOD 26`).

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The "Max Pain Is Not Support" Law (`CB-01`):** A common retail trap is buying Bitcoin blindly because *"Max Pain is $77,000 and BTC is at $71,000, so dealers will push it back up by Friday."* As November 10–13, 2026 proved, when macro tightening (`10Y Yield 5.13%`) and Spot ETF outflows (`-$1.44B`) hit simultaneously, spot breaks right through put walls and leaves Max Pain far above. **Never trade Max Pain without Pillar 1 permission and Pillar 2 USD Spot CVD confirmation.**
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Pre-Expiry Pinning Chop Filter (`CB-03`):** When macro conditions *are* calm (`MOVE < 85`, `Positive GEX`), massive monthly/quarterly expiries (`> 80K BTC` OI) create strong dealer hedging gravity within `+/- 3%` of Max Pain during the final 36–48 hours—chopping up breakout traders until `08:00 UTC` Friday passes.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Post-Expiry Volatility Release (`CB-04`):** Once the `08:00 UTC` Friday settlement bell rings, billions of dollars in option gamma expire simultaneously. Dealers unwind their delta hedges, frequently unleashing a clean directional trend move over the following Friday NY and Monday sessions.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-26A: Post-OpEx Volatility Release Breakout ("Gamma Unclench Impulse")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. A major monthly or quarterly Deribit/CME OpEx (`>= 60,000 BTC` expiring OI) has just settled (`after Friday 08:00 UTC` / `16:00 UTC`).
  2. During the week leading into OpEx, price was pinned in a tight range (`Garman-Klass volatility <= 25th percentile`, `MOD 12`).

* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Expiring options removed the dealer gamma ceiling that was suppressing price; institutional spot buyers step into a freshly cleared derivatives slate.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Clean multi-day directional trend expansion (`+6% to +15%`) as new monthly option positioning builds at higher strikes.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on the first 15m/1H pullback retest of the **reclaimed pre-expiry VAH** after Friday `08:00 UTC` settlement, sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling**.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at `+2.5R` or next major new-month Call Wall; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H Volume Profile VAL.

### CSU-26B: Calm-Regime Pre-OpEx Max Pain Gravitation ("Controlled Mean-Reversion Drift")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Time-to-expiry is **`T-48h to T-6h`** before a major Friday `08:00 UTC` expiry (`>= 40,000 BTC` OI).
  2. Pillar 1 is calm (`GATE_GREEN` or `GATE_AMBER`, `MOVE < 85`, no major CPI/FOMC shock), and spot trades `2.0% to 4.5%` below **Max Pain** while sitting cleanly above a major **Put Wall / 4H VAL**.
  3. **Domestic USD Spot CVD** turns positive at 4H VAL support (`confirming real buyers alongside dealer delta-hedging`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

  * **Probable Trajectory:** Controlled mean-reversion drift toward Max Pain, stalling right at the Max Pain strike before settlement.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Reduced-Size Unleveraged Spot Long (`0.375% Risk`)** upon 15m/1H VAL reclaim, with **mandatory Stage 2 Full Profit Exit at Max Pain (or by Friday `07:30 UTC` ahead of settlement)**.

### CSU-26C: Pre-OpEx Pinning Chop ("T-24H Dealer Equilibrium Lock")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Time-to-expiry is **`< 24 hours`** before a major monthly/quarterly OpEx (`>= 80,000 BTC` OI), and spot is already trading within **`+/- 1.2%` of Max Pain**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Dealer gamma pinning is at peak intensity; every `0.8%` move up or down is immediately faded by automated market-maker hedging.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY IN 100% CASH (`CB-03`).** Zero new entries allowed until after `08:00 UTC` Friday settlement clears the pin.

### CSU-26D: Macro Override Breakdown Through OpEx ("Unpinned Waterfall")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Ahead of Friday OpEx, Pillar 1 trips `GATE_RED` (`10Y yield / DXY / ETF outflow shock`) and spot breaks below both Max Pain and the primary **Put Wall** (`MOD 25C`).
