## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Sign Convention Standardization (`CB-02`):** Different analytics dashboards display 25-Delta Skew with opposite signs (`Put IV - Call IV` vs. `Call IV - Put IV`). In **Project CORTEX-BTC**, we standardize strictly on **`Skew_25D = IV(25Δ Put) − IV(25Δ Call)`**:
  * **High Positive (`+7% to +15%`):** Downside puts are vastly more expensive than upside calls (**Institutional Fear / Crash Hedging**).
  * **Deep Negative (`-4% to -10%`):** Upside calls are vastly more expensive than downside puts (**Retail/Fund Call FOMO Euphoria**).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Volatility Term Structure Backwardation Lock (`CB-03`):** Normally, 90-day IV is higher than 7-day IV (`Contango`). When 7-day ATM IV spikes sharply *above* 90-day ATM IV (`Backwardation`), options dealers are pricing immediate near-term crisis risk. Lock **100% Cash** until front-end IV cools back down.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Skew Peak Inflection Law (`CB-01`):** Extreme put skew (`>= +8%`) identifies a crowded hedge, **not** an instant entry. You enter *only* when put skew stops making higher highs and begins rolling over downward alongside positive **Domestic USD Spot CVD**.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-27A: Extreme Put-Skew Panic Inflection ("Over-Hedged Capitulation Bottom")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `7D / 30D Skew_25D` spikes into the **`>= 92nd rolling percentile` (`+7.5% to +14.0%+`)** and Term Structure enters backwardation (`Front IV > Back IV`), reflecting panic institutional put buying at the lows.
  2. **Inflection Proof:** `Skew_25D` peaks and turns **downward (`dropping >= -2.0 vol points off peak`)** while price sweeps HTF support (`MOD 03A / 11A`) and **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** turns positive.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional funds have finished buying downside crash insurance; as spot stabilizes, put-option decay (`Vanna & Charm` dealer flows) forces market makers to **buy back short futures hedges**, fueling a powerful reflexive rebound.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sharp multi-day relief rally (`+6% to +14%`) powered by Vanna/Charm dealer short-covering and spot accumulation.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon confirmed **15m/1H VAL or VAH reclaim** coinciding with `Skew_25D` rolling downward off its panic peak and positive USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% once `Skew_25D` normalizes back to neutral (`+2% to +3%`) and Term Structure returns to contango; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H Volume Profile VAL.

### CSU-27B: Euphoric Call-Skew Blow-Off ("Upside Tail-Chasing Ceiling")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `7D / 30D Skew_25D` plunges into deep negative territory (**`<= -4.5% to -8.0%`**, `<= 8th rolling percentile`—calls trading at massive implied volatility premiums over puts).
  2. Coincides with high positive perp funding (`MOD 02B`) and flattening **Domestic USD Spot CVD** at overhead resistance / Call Wall.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Speculators are aggressively buying out-of-the-money calls to chase upside leverage while downside protection is completely neglected.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High probability of a sharp mean-reversion pullback (`-6% to -15%`) that crushes call premiums and flushes late longs.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **EXECUTE STAGE 2 FULL PROFIT EXIT (100% OF OPEN SPOT SWING).** Lock Cash and ban new long entries until call skew normalizes.

### CSU-27C: Multi-Year DVOL Compression Complacency ("Unhedged Air-Pocket Warning")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Deribit DVOL** compresses into the **`<= 10th rolling 1Y percentile` (`e.g., DVOL ~38–42`, benchmark 39.8 in Nov 2026)** with complacent low put skew (`< +2.5%`), **while** Pillar 1 macro catalysts (CPI, FOMC, rising 10Y yields) are approaching.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The market is asleep and under-pricing macro tail risk; because dealers and funds carry minimal downside option protection, any negative macro surprise will trigger frantic spot/futures hedging sales (`gap-down air pocket`).
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Explosive upcoming volatility expansion (`DVOL surge from ~40 toward 55–65+`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **DO NOT CHASE LOW-VOLATILITY BREAKOUTS AHEAD OF MACRO PRINTS.** Enforce `MOD 12A` discipline—wait for the DVOL expansion move to print and confirm direction via Domestic USD Spot CVD before deploying capital.

### CSU-27D: Healthy Bull-Trend Skew Equilibrium ("Balanced Contango Drift")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `DVOL` sits in a moderate `45–60` range, Volatility Term Structure is in smooth **Contango** (`Back IV > Front IV` by `+2 to +5 pts`), and `Skew_25D` holds steady between **`-1.5% and +2.5%`**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Black Swan & Custody Defense)

When an exogenous Black Swan event strikes (an exchange hack, stablecoin de-peg, sudden regulatory ban, or sovereign debt crisis), standard technical patterns break down for 24–72 hours. Module 28 enforces four non-negotiable survival laws:
* **Black Swan Law 1 — Never Buy the First 4 Hours of an Exchange Hack (`BS-01`):** Historical forensics across the **Bybit ($1.46B)**, **Bitget ($387.5M)**, and **Binance ($6.3B)** incidents prove that initial panic wicks are followed by multi-day contagion fears, withdrawal freezes, and forced institutional de-risking. Lock **100% Cash** and move exchange balances to **multi-vendor hardware multi-sig cold storage** immediately.
* **Black Swan Law 2 — The Stablecoin Flight Mirage (`BS-02`):** When a major stablecoin (`USDT`) de-pegs downward (`$0.97–$0.99`), traders panic-buy `BTC/USDT` to exit Tether—making `BTC/USDT` appear to skyrocket while `BTC/USD` (Coinbase) drops. Always benchmark against true `USD` pairs and stand down until the peg restores.
* **Black Swan Law 3 — Mandatory Post-Loss Autopsy (`BS-04`):** Any time the system experiences two consecutive stop-outs (`-1.50%` cumulative risk) or misses a major regime shift, trading halts for **72 hours** until we execute the 4-Step Autopsy Protocol below and commit a new `MOD 29+` source to [PROJECT CORTEX-BTC](https://gemini.google.com/notebook/67505219-8bb5-4ba3-b9da-5d3ae67348ad).

### CSU-28C: Post-Trade Loss / Regime-Miss Autopsy ("Epistemic Compounding Loop")

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Discount-Rate Denominator Law (`CB-01`):** Retail traders assume strong corporate earnings (`+30% YoY`) guarantee a bull market. In reality, when central banks hold real interest rates restrictive, the mathematical discount-rate denominator expands—crushing P/E multiples (`-20%`). Because Bitcoin trades as a high-beta liquidity/duration proxy during macro tightening, equity multiple compression creates an invisible ceiling over BTC rallies.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Energy Cost-Push Stagflation Trap (`CB-03`):** Surging crude oil (`Brent > $108`, `WTI > $100`) leaks directly into headline CPI and PPI (`e.g., PPI 6.3%–6.6% YoY`), tying the Federal Reserve's hands and preventing rate cuts even if equities fall.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — AI Capex & Tech Credit Contagion (`CB-02`):** When AI hyperscaler credit spreads widen (`e.g., 15% neocloud bond yields`) or semiconductor indices (`SOX`) suffer multi-day liquidation streaks, institutional multi-asset funds sell liquid Bitcoin holdings to cover tech/equity drawdowns.

* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**

  2. **Equity/Credit Stress:** `SOX` or `Nasdaq-100` plunges `>= -8%` alongside widening AI/corporate credit spreads or global index circuit breakers (e.g., KOSPI drawdown).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-beta liquidation contagion dragging Bitcoin toward deep on-chain support shelves.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

### CSU-29C: Passive Treasury-Proxy Index Exclusion ("Forced Mechanical Selling Window")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Major index provider (MSCI, S&P, Nasdaq) announces removal or re-weighting of a major Bitcoin corporate treasury equity (`e.g., MSTR`), creating a known passive-fund forced rebalancing deadline.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Passive index funds must sell shares mechanically regardless of price, depressing equity-crypto sentiment and triggering sympathetic futures shorting in BTC.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

### CSU-29D: Discount-Rate Pivot & Multiple Expansion Ignition ("Liquidity Re-Rating")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Oil retreats below `$85/bbl`, inflation prints cool below consensus, `10Y` real yields drop sharply, and equity P/E multiples begin expanding alongside positive **US Spot ETF inflows**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (PCF3 Engine Governance)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Spot-Over-Perp Dominance Law:** Across all 10 PCF3 configurations, **Spot CVD + Spot Volume + ETF Flows** act as the primary truth filter. Whenever `Perp CVD` and `Open Interest` dominate while `Spot CVD` is weak or diverging (`Configs B, D, H`), the move is classified as **synthetic fragility**—never chase it.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Macro Headwind Override (`Config C` vs. `Config D`):** When macro headwinds are high (`Pillar 1` stressed), **Config C (`Bullish Absorption`)** proves Bitcoin is absorbing macro pressure via genuine spot/ETF buying, whereas **Config D (`Masked Fragility`)** warns that a thin-book perp rally is hiding imminent collapse.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Cascade Stand-Down (`Config E` vs. `Config F`):** Never buy during **Config E (`Long Liquidation Cascade`)**; wait strictly until telemetry transitions into **Config F (`Post-Cascade Reset`)** or **Config J (`Bear Trap / Absorption`)**.

| **`CONFIG_C`** | **BULLISH ABSORPTION** | **High macro headwinds** + Price $\uparrow$ + **USD Spot CVD $\uparrow$** + Spot Vol expanding + ETF inflows $\uparrow$ + OI controlled + Funding neutral. | Institutional spot demand is actively absorbing macro selling pressure—true relative strength. | **Authorize `0.375%–0.75% Risk` Spot Long** upon confirmed 4H VAH/VWAP acceptance. |
| **`CONFIG_D`** | **MASKED FRAGILITY** | **High macro headwinds** + Price $\uparrow$ + Spot Vol weak + **USD Spot CVD weak** + Perp CVD spiking + OI exploding + Funding spiking + **Book liquidity thinning**. | Bullish price optics masking severe underlying fragility—imminent rug-pull trap. | **ENFORCE 100% CASH LOCK.** Exit any tactical longs immediately. |
| **`CONFIG_E`** | **LONG LIQUIDATION CASCADE** | Price $\downarrow$ + **Long liquidations spiking** + **OI collapsing** + Funding collapsing + USD Spot CVD negative + Book liquidity deteriorating. | Active forced deleveraging waterfall in progress. | **MANDATORY 100% CASH FREEZE (`CB-03`).** Zero knife-catching until OI stops falling. |
| **`CONFIG_F`** | **POST-CASCADE RESET** | Large long liquidation completed $\rightarrow$ **OI falls substantially (`-8%+`)** $\rightarrow$ Funding normalizes $\rightarrow$ Price stabilizes $\rightarrow$ **USD Spot CVD turns $\uparrow$** + Spot Vol returns. | Leverage purged and organic spot demand stepping in; transition from cascade to re-accumulation. | **Authorize `0.375%–0.75% Risk` Spot Long** on 15m/1H VAL reclaim (`R >= 3.0`). |
| **`CONFIG_G`** | **SHORT SQUEEZE** | Price spiking $\uparrow$ + **Short liquidations spiking** + Funding initially neutral/negative + **OI falls initially**. | Trapped shorts forced to cover (`MOD 01A`). *Watch:* Promotes to `CONFIG_A` only if USD Spot CVD + Vol expand afterward. | Hold open squeeze long; execute **Stage 1 (50%) exit** into climax if USD Spot CVD fails to follow through. |
| **`CONFIG_H`** | **DISTRIBUTION / TOPPING RISK** | Price at highs **BUT** **USD Spot CVD $\downarrow$** + Spot Vol $\downarrow$ + ETF flows deteriorating + **OI exploding + Funding $\uparrow$ + Perp CVD $\uparrow$ + Whale exchange deposits $\uparrow$ + VWAP rejection**. | Maximum institutional distribution into retail leverage FOMO (`top warning`). | **EXECUTE 100% FULL PROFIT EXIT.** Lock Cash immediately. |
| **`CONFIG_I`** | **BEARISH CONTINUATION** | Price $\downarrow$ + **USD Spot CVD $\downarrow$** + Spot Vol expanding on selling + OI elevated + Funding positive + Long liquidations $\uparrow$ + **VWAP rejection**. | Structural downtrend confirmed by both spot selling and trapped dip-buyers (`MOD 04B`). | **STRICT 100% CASH LOCK.** |
| **`CONFIG_J`** | **BEAR TRAP / ABSORPTION** | Price $\downarrow$ on negative headlines + Spot CVD initially $\downarrow$, **BUT** sell volume declines + OI collapses + Long liquidations peak + Price stops falling + **USD Spot CVD turns $\uparrow$ + Reclaims VWAP**. | Bearish catalyst fully absorbed at liquidity pool (`Wyckoff Spring`); high-EV reversal setup. | **Authorize `0.375%–0.75% Risk` Spot Long** upon confirmed VWAP/VAL reclaim with stop below sweep wick. |

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-31A: Low Fragility / Robust Bids (`ICDM Score = 0 to 35 Points`)

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Custody & Counterparty Defense)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Solvency Ratio Filter (`M_solv`):** Not every exchange hack is a systemic FTX-style collapse. By computing `Loss_To_Fund_Ratio = Stolen_USD / Verified_Insurance_Fund_USD`, you avoid panic-selling core spot BTC during a **ring-fenced shock (`Ratio <= 1.0`)**, while immediately locking **100% Cash** and withdrawing during a **systemic insolvency breach (`Ratio > 1.0`)**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — The 72-Hour Operational Wall:** Exchange accounts are strictly execution venues, never vaults. Active trading capital on exchanges is ring-fenced to tactical swing allocation, while sovereign cold storage enforces a **72-hour multi-sig operational time-lock / verification protocol** against coercion or impulsive panic transfers.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Zero-Trust Hardware & Firmware Hygiene:** Eliminate single-vendor firmware bugs, supply-chain tampering, and vendor random-number-generator (RNG) backdoors by requiring **multi-vendor 2-of-3 multi-sig**, **physical dice-roll offline entropy**, and **offline GPG signature verification** on every firmware binary.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Quantum & Address-Reuse Defense:** Never reuse Bitcoin addresses; keeping unspent UTXOs behind unrevealed public-key hashes (`P2WSH` multi-sig) protects cold storage against theoretical future ECDSA/quantum key-recovery vectors.

```yaml
title: "AEGIS-3: 3-Stage Impulse Defense & Anti-Capitulation Sell-Freeze"

# THREE-STAGE EXECUTION CLOCKS
evaluation_clocks:
  stage_1_sfi_pre_event: "1H / 4H cycle + mandatory check 60 min before low-liquidity transitions (23:00 UTC / 07:00 MYT)"
  stage_2_ilvd_streaming: "1m / 5m velocity + Rough Path Levy signed area + asynchronous wire NLP"
  stage_3_exhaustion_lock: "5m / 15m post-impulse liquidation decay & bid replenishment gate"

# STAGE 1: STRUCTURAL FRAGILITY INDEX (SFI in [0, 1])
sfi_thresholds:
  NORMAL_RESILIENT: "SFI < 0.45 -> 100% of strategy target permitted; baseline Stage 2 sensitivity"
  ELEVATED_FRAGILITY: "0.45 <= SFI < 0.70 -> Tighten trailing stops; cap new entries; tighten Stage 2 velocity threshold by 25%"
  CRITICAL_FRAGILITY_PRE_TRIM: "SFI >= 0.70 -> Proactively pre-trim 25%-40% of active spot exposure via TWAP slices during liquid hours"

# STAGE 2: IMPULSE & LIQUIDITY VACUUM DETECTOR (ILVD)
ilvd_triggers:
  session_normalized_velocity_zr: "Z_r = (r_dt - mu_TOD) / sigma_TOD <= -2.2 (relaxed to -1.7 when SFI >= 0.70)"
  nlp_wire_multiplier: "Asynchronous geopolitical/regulatory/halt wire velocity lowers Z_r threshold by 0.3 sigma"
  execution_mechanics: "Slippage-bounded chase slices: 3-5 child limit orders pegged 2-5 bps inside best bid over 5-10 min, re-pegging every 15s (zero blind market dumps)"

# STAGE 3: HARD SELL-FREEZE (CIRCUIT_LOCK = TRUE)
circuit_lock_conditions_any_two:
  - "Rolling 4H cross-exchange long liquidations > 95th historical percentile"
  - "Intraday drawdown from UTC open > -3.8% within < 4 hours alongside > 3.5 sigma 15m volatility spike"
  - "Spot order-book bid replenishment ratio > 1.2x ask replenishment after psychological level sweep"

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
  exit_protocol: "AEGIS3_BOUNDED_LIMIT_SLICING"
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (AEGIS-3 Operational Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — State-Before-Catalyst Asymmetry (`CB-01`):** Exogenous headlines (even unverified geopolitical claims, such as the Nov 13 IRGC Bahrain radar report) do not cause vertical crashes on their own; they detonate pre-existing liquidity-starved books. Defensive pre-trimming (`Stage 1 SFI >= 0.70`) must execute *before* low-liquidity session transitions (`23:00 UTC / 07:00 MYT`) when ETF outflows and thin depth converge.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — No Blind Market Dumps in Thin Books (`CB-02`):** During active liquidity vacuums, raw market sell orders cross empty order-book ticks and suffer severe slippage. All `Stage 2` exits must execute via **bounded limit-chase slices** (`3–5` child slices pegged `2–5 bps` inside best bid, re-pegging every `15 seconds`).
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Anti-Capitulation Sell-Freeze (`CB-03 / CIRCUIT_LOCK`):** Once forced long liquidations blow off (`~3 to 4 hours` after initial wire trigger), expected value flips violently from selling to waiting. `CIRCUIT_LOCK` physically blocks automated or discretionary spot selling at the exhaustion trough.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Staged Post-Flush Re-Entry (`CB-04 / RECOVERY_GATE`):** Preserved cash remains locked until 1H liquidation velocity drops below `25%` of cascade peak for 2 consecutive hours and 1H Domestic USD Spot CVD turns net positive.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-34A: Pre-Event Critical Fragility (`SFI >= 0.70` — `PRE_TRIM` State)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Normalized **Structural Fragility Index ($\text{SFI} \ge 0.70$)** across four components: multi-day US Spot ETF net outflows + contracting stablecoin supply ($x_1$), top-of-book `1%–2%` bid depth well below 30D session median ($x_2$), rising perp OI/funding against negative Spot CVD ($x_3$), and defensive 25D put skew near dense long liquidation shelves ($x_4$).
  2. Approaching low-liquidity session handoff (`23:00 UTC / 07:00 MYT` or weekend close).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Bid-side cushion is hollow; any unverified wire or macro surprise can trigger an 85-minute vertical cascade.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **PROACTIVELY PRE-TRIM `25%–40%` OF OPEN SPOT EXPOSURE** via passive/TWAP slices during liquid NY/overlap hours before volatility spikes; block all new swing entries.

### CSU-34B: Impulse & Liquidity Vacuum Breakdown (`ILVD` Fires — `IMPULSE_EXIT` State)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Session-Normalized Return Velocity ($Z_r \le -2.2$, or $\le -1.7$ when $\text{SFI} \ge 0.70$)** on 15m/30m log returns (`further relaxed by 0.3 sigma` if asynchronous wire NLP detects geopolitical escalation, exchange halt, or regulatory shock).
  2. **Order-Flow Confirmation:** Aggressive market-sell delta accelerates on Spot CVD while Order $N \le 2$ **Rough Path Lévy Signed Area ($\mathcal{A}_{p,v}$)** turns sharply negative (`MOD 08`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Normal two-way chop has mutated into a one-sided liquidity vacuum ahead of major psychological stop clusters (`e.g., $70,000`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **EXECUTE BOUNDED LIMIT-CHASE EXIT (`0%–25%` target exposure remaining)** using `3–5` sliced limit orders pegged `2–5 bps` inside best bid over `5–10 minutes` (re-pegging every `15s`).

### CSU-34C: Capitulation Blow-Off & Hard Sell-Freeze (`CIRCUIT_LOCK = TRUE`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Any **two of three** exhaustion conditions trigger simultaneously during a sharp drop:
     * Rolling 4H long liquidations exceed the **95th historical percentile**.
     * Intraday drawdown from UTC open exceeds **`-3.8%` within `< 4 hours`** alongside a **`> 3.5 sigma`** 15m volatility spike.
     * Spot order-book **bid replenishment ratio exceeds `1.2x` ask replenishment** after sweeping a psychological level.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Forced margin selling has reached peak climax (`typically ~3.5 hours after initial headline wire`). Selling here locks in the exact bottom tick.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENGAGE HARD SELL-FREEZE (`CIRCUIT_LOCK = TRUE`).** Zero market or limit selling permitted; transition immediately to `RECOVERY_GATE` monitoring.

### CSU-34D: Post-Cascade Stabilization & Tranched Redeployment (`RECOVERY_GATE`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. 1H liquidation rate decays below **`25%` of cascade peak** for **2 consecutive hours**.
  2. 1H **Domestic USD Spot CVD** flattens and turns net positive while price holds above the cascade trough.
  3. Core `MOD 18 / MOD 33` waterfall confirms valid entry confluence (`R >= 3.0`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Passive limit buyers have absorbed the cascade; clean post-flush base is forming (`MOD 30 CONFIG_F`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **REDEPLOY PRESERVED CASH IN 2–3 TRANCHES** capped strictly within the `0.75%` (`or 0.375% Amber`) Sovereign Risk Shield.

| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |

# [39/44] MOD 35 — Liquidation Heatmap Distance Rule   Pull Score Engine

---

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 35: LIQUIDATION HEATMAP DISTANCE RULE & PULL SCORE ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 & Pillar 3 Quantitative Liquidity-Target & Permission Engine (Ranking Competing Upper vs. Lower Liquidation Magnets).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from `The Distance Rule` (`CS4`) and `The Liquidity-Hunt Myth` (`CS1` — Nov 13, 2026 BTC worked case study), establishing exact percentage-distance normalization, the **Pull Score** ranking formula, ATR movement feasibility, and the **Permission-Before-Target** law.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_35"
entry_type: "LIQUIDITY_MAGNET_PULL_SCORER"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_2_3_HEATMAP_PULL_SCORE_ENGINE"
title: "Liquidation Heatmap Distance Rule & Pull Score Engine"

# CORE MATHEMATICAL FORMULAS
distance_pct_formula: "Distance_Pct = ((Magnet_Price - Current_Price) / Current_Price) * 100.0"
pull_score_formula: "Pull_Score = Cluster_Size_USD_M / abs(Distance_Pct)"
atr_distance_formula: "ATR_Multiples = abs(Magnet_Price - Current_Price) / Daily_ATR_14"

# THE 5 DISTANCE RULES & SKEW THRESHOLDS
distance_skew_thresholds:
  LOPSIDED_PULL_DOMINANCE: "Nearest_Pull_Score > 1.5 * Opposing_Pull_Score -> Expect visit to cheaper/nearer magnet first"
  BALANCED_CHOP_ZONE: "Upper_Distance_Pct ~= Lower_Distance_Pct (both 2%-3% away, ratio < 1.5x) -> Expect ping-pong chop (Standby)"
  FAR_MAGNET_CATALYST_GATE: "Distance_Pct >= 6.0% OR ATR_Multiples >= 2.0 -> Requires exogenous catalyst + Permission Gate"

# PERMISSION-BEFORE-TARGET GATE (FALSIFYING THE LIQUIDITY-HUNT MYTH)
permission_gate_requirements:
  - "Heatmap intensity is unitless model output (CoinGlass Docs), NEVER resting limit orders or a timetable"
  - "Upside short-liquidation magnet requires Squeeze Fuel (negative funding + OI stacked at lows) OR Spot CVD + Coinbase Premium >= 0"
  - "Downside long-liquidation magnet dominates whenever trend is down, funding >= 0, and OI rises into drops (Cascade Fuel)"

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Heatmap Anti-Myth Governance)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Heatmap Category-Error Filter (`CB-01`):** Liquidation heatmaps (e.g., CoinGlass Models 1/2/3) display *model-estimated* liquidation concentrations, **not** resting order-book limit orders and **not** a schedule. Never treat bright overhead yellow bands as guaranteed upside magnets when spot flow and structure point down (empirical proof: Nov 6–13, 2026, where a bright `~$76.0k–$76.5k` week-old band sat untouched while BTC fell `-7%` to `$70.5k`).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Energy Cost & Intervening Friction Filter (`CB-02`):** Every `1%` price move requires net order-flow fuel and must clear intermediate structural shelves. A distant `$200M` cluster `+13%` (`~2.5 ATR`) away is far weaker in the near term than a `$32.5M` cluster `0.5%` (`< 0.3 ATR`) away.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Permission Over Magnet (`CB-03`):** Direction is granted exclusively by **Structure + Spot/Perp CVD + Positioning (`Funding / OI`)**. Heatmaps only supply *conditional targets* once flow grants permission to travel in that direction.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Never Buy Before the Nearer Downside Flush (`CB-04`):** When lower Pull Score exceeds upper Pull Score by `> 1.5x` and longs are crowded (`funding >= 0`, `top-trader L/S > 1.8`), never front-run an upside hunt; wait for the cheaper downside cluster to flush first.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-35A: Downside Pull Dominance ("Cheap Downside Trip / Cascade Fuel")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Pull Score Skew:** $\text{Pull Score}_{\text{Lower}} > 1.5 \times \text{Pull Score}_{\text{Upper}}$ (benchmark: Nov 13, 2026 at `$69,223`, where `$68,235` lower magnet had `Pull = 65.0` vs. `$73,635` upper magnet `Pull = 14.4` — a **`4.5x` downside pull advantage**).
  2. **Cascade Fuel Active:** Price in 1D/4H downtrend, `funding >= 0.00%`, `OI` rising or elevated into drops, and `USD Spot CVD` / `Coinbase Premium` negative.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Downside cluster is `0.3–1.0 ATR` away with zero intervening support, while upside magnets sit `2.0–2.6 ATR` away behind multiple resistance shelves.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Market takes the "cheap trip" down to sweep nearby long liquidations first; minor `0.2 ATR` bounces (`+0.5%`) are noise, not reversals.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE `100% CASH LOCK` (`0.00%` Risk).** Block all anticipatory dip-buying until the lower cluster sweeps and `MOD 03 / MOD 34` exhaustion confirms.

### CSU-35B: Upside Pull Dominance with Squeeze Permission ("Coiled Short-Squeeze Path")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Pull Score Skew:** $\text{Pull Score}_{\text{Upper}} > 1.5 \times \text{Pull Score}_{\text{Lower}}$ (nearest short-liquidation cluster within `0.5%–2.0%` / `< 0.8 ATR` overhead with clean LVN path).
  2. **Permission Granted (`Squeeze Fuel`):** `Funding <= -0.015%` with `OI` holding/rising at lows (crowded shorts paying), **OR** price reclaims broken 4H support/VAH with rising `USD Spot CVD` and `Coinbase Premium >= 0.00%`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Short distance + crowded paying shorts creates self-reinforcing forced buy-to-cover acceleration once the nearest cluster triggers.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **AUTHORIZE `0.375%–0.75% Risk` SPOT LONG** upon confirmed 1H/4H boundary reclaim (`R >= 3.0`), using the upper magnet chain (`T1` nearest cluster, `T2` secondary cluster) for `Stage 1 (50%)` profit harvesting.

### CSU-35C: Balanced Magnet Symmetry ("Ping-Pong Equilibrium Chop")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Nearest upper and lower magnets sit at roughly equal distances (`2.0%–3.0%` each way) with $\frac{\max(\text{Pull}_{\text{Upper}}, \text{Pull}_{\text{Lower}})}{\min(\text{Pull}_{\text{Upper}}, \text{Pull}_{\text{Lower}})} < 1.5$.
  2. Neutral funding, flat OI, and Garman-Klass volatility compressed (`MOD 12`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Neither side offers an asymmetric energy advantage; price oscillates between boundaries while heatmap clusters decay and redraw.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY IN CASH (`0.00%` Mid-Range Exposure).** Trade only verified extreme Value Area sweeps (`MOD 11A / 11C`).

### CSU-35D: Far-Magnet Catalyst Ignition ("Multi-ATR Expansion Run")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Major dense liquidity band sits `>= 6.0%` (`>= 2.0 ATR`) away behind intermediate resistance shelves, **AND** an exogenous macro/ETF catalyst (cool CPI/PPI, Fed liquidity pivot, `> +$250M` ETF inflow surge) triggers a high-volume 4H close above the nearest intervening shelf (`MOD 11B`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Microstructure Lens:** Exogenous catalyst supplies the multi-ATR energy required to clear intermediate resistance levels and reach the distant cluster.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **HOLD STAGE 2 (`50%`) TRAILING RUNNER** toward the distant magnet once `Stage 1 (50%)` is banked at the first cleared shelf.

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |

| **M2: Downside Sweep $\rightarrow$ Permission Flip** | `CSU-35A` (Lower Sweep) | Lower magnet sweeps on volume climax, **funding flips negative (`< -0.015%`), OI drops `-8%+`, and 4H reclaims broken support**. | Downside cascade fuel is cleared and flipped into upside squeeze fuel (`Permission Granted`). | **Transition to `CSU-35B`:** Execute `0.375%–0.75% Risk` Spot Long targeting upper magnet chain. |
| **M3: Noise Tag Rejection (`0.2 ATR` Trap)** | `CSU-35A` (Downtrend) | Price bounces `+0.6%` (`0.2 ATR`) to tag a tiny fresh 24h short cluster (`e.g., $71.0k`), then **rejects at 4H resistance with negative Spot CVD**. | Routine downtrend noise squeezing chase-shorts—not a structural reversal. | **Ignore the Bounce;** maintain `100% Cash Lock`. |

| **M6: Crowd Flip Mid-Range** | `CSU-35C` (Chop) | While price chops mid-range, **funding plunges to `-0.025%` and OI jumps `+6%`** (aggressive short buildup at range lows). | Positioning skewed heavily short; upside Pull Score + Permission now align. | **Upgrade to `CSU-35B` Watch;** prepare `MOD 11A` VAL reclaim long trigger. |
| **M7: Multi-Model Heatmap Divergence** | Daily Mapping | CoinGlass Model 1 (`high leverage`) shows a nearby cluster, while Model 2/3 (`low leverage`) shows the true heavy shelf `3%` deeper. | High-leverage bucket is decoy fuel; true institutional liquidation wall sits at the Model 2/3 shelf. | **Anchor Invalidation Stop Beneath the Deeper Model 2/3 Shelf** (`minus 0.6%` buffer). |
| **M8: Morning Distance Worksheet Audit** | Daily `08:00 MYT` Scan | Compute `Distance %` and `Pull Score` for nearest 2 upper & 2 lower magnets alongside `MOD 31` ICDM score. | Prevents narrative bias ("inevitable pump") by quantifying exact ATR & percentage energy cost. | **Enforce Distance Rule 1:** Always plan around the higher Pull Score side unless a catalyst fires. |

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 36: TELEMETRY TRUST, STALENESS SLA & DETERMINISM ATTESTATION
**Waterfall Tier:** Tier D Supreme Control-Plane Gate (Sits *above* `MOD 33` Master Regime Radar and `MOD 18` Sizing Engine to enforce fail-closed data integrity, cross-venue price quorum, cron heartbeat verification, and bit-for-bit replay determinism).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT, Qwen, Perplexity) — addressing GitHub Actions `schedule` best-effort top-of-hour queue delays/drops, stale API feeds, cross-venue price outliers, and strict separation between NotebookLM qualitative research and the deterministic pure-Python compiler (`cortex_matrix_engine.py`).

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_36"
entry_type: "CONTROL_PLANE_TRUST_ATTESTATION"
waterfall_pillar: "TIER_D_SUPREME_TELEMETRY_TRUST_GATE"
title: "Telemetry Trust, Staleness SLA & Determinism Attestation"

# SUPREME HIERARCHY POSITION
governance_priority: 1 # Overrides MOD 33 Regime Permission and MOD 18 Entry Sizing
icdm_contribution_mode: "BINARY_FAIL_CLOSED_GATE" # Does not add ICDM points; emits GREEN / AMBER / RED / UNKNOWN

# MANDATORY MODULE OUTPUT CONTRACT (APPLIES TO ALL MODULES)
common_module_contract:
  required_timestamps: ["event_timestamp_utc", "publication_timestamp_utc", "ingestion_timestamp_utc", "as_of_timestamp_utc"]
  required_hashes: ["ruleset_yaml_sha256", "input_snapshot_sha256", "decision_replay_sha256"]
  allowed_states: ["GREEN", "AMBER", "RED", "UNKNOWN"]
  zero_hallucination_rule: "UNKNOWN (missing/stale/unverified) MUST NEVER be coerced to 0 or GREEN."

# TELEMETRY SLA & QUORUM THRESHOLDS
sla_and_quorum_thresholds:
  feed_staleness_multiplier_max: 2.0 # Feed age > 2.0x declared cadence -> UNKNOWN / TRUST_DEGRADED
  cron_heartbeat_max_gap_minutes: 90 # GitHub Actions hourly schedule dead-man switch
  venue_quorum_min_sources: 3 # Minimum independent spot venues (e.g., Coinbase, Binance, Kraken/Bitstamp)
  venue_quorum_max_dispersion_bps: 35.0 # Max mid-price spread across quorum venues before outlier freeze
  ntp_clock_skew_max_ms: 2000 # Max local clock drift vs UTC NTP reference
  rearm_clean_cycles_required: 2 # Consecutive clean cycles required to lift TRUST_DEGRADED lock

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  degraded_trust_max_risk_pct: 0.00 # Strict Fail-Closed Cash / Freeze
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Control-Plane Data Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Fail-Closed `UNKNOWN` Law (`CB-01`):** Missing, stale, or unverified telemetry must be tagged `UNKNOWN`—never defaulted to `0.0` or `GREEN`. While `RED` logs a verified adverse market event and `UNKNOWN` logs an unverified telemetry state, **both block all new spot entries**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — GitHub Actions Cron Dead-Man Switch (`CB-02`):** Because GitHub Actions `schedule` triggers (`cron: "5 * * * *"`) are best-effort and subject to top-of-hour queue delays or drops under load, an independent watchdog checks `ingestion_timestamp_utc`. If no attested pipeline heartbeat arrives within **`90 minutes`**, new entries lock automatically until **2 consecutive clean cycles** pass.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Three-Venue Price Quorum (`CB-03`):** Spot reference price and stop-loss triggers must be validated across at least **3 independent spot venues** (e.g., Coinbase USD, Binance USDT, Kraken/Bitstamp USD). If dispersion exceeds **`35 bps`**, freeze execution to prevent single-venue API glitches or flash-wick anomalies from triggering false entries or premature stops.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Bit-for-Bit Compiler Determinism (`CB-04`):** NotebookLM and LLM summaries are strictly research/audit layers and **never** enter the live execution path. `cortex_matrix_engine.py` must log `ruleset_yaml_sha256` + `input_snapshot_sha256` so any decision can be replayed bit-for-bit during `MOD 28` Live Autopsies.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-36A: Critical Feed Staleness or Cron Heartbeat Lapse (`TRUST_DEGRADED — Fail-Closed`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Any critical input feed (`spot price`, `USD/Perp CVD`, `OI/funding`, or `order-book depth`) exceeds **`2.0x` its declared cadence SLA**, **OR** `as_of_timestamp_utc` lags current UTC time by **`> 90 minutes`** (missed/delayed cron heartbeat).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** The engine is blind to current market state; computing entries on stale data creates unpriced tail exposure.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **SET `MOD_36_STATE = UNKNOWN (TRUST_DEGRADED)`.** Override `MOD 33` to block all new spot entries; manage any existing open swing strictly from the last attested pre-computed stop/target levels; send `CRITICAL` Telegram HUD warning.

### CSU-36B: Cross-Venue Quorum Dispersion Breach ("Single-Venue Feed Anomaly")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Fewer than **`3` independent spot venues** respond within timeout, **OR** the max-minus-min mid-price spread across active quorum venues exceeds **`35 bps`** (`0.35%`) outside a verified `MOD 32` exchange solvency event.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** One venue is experiencing an API freeze, order-book outage, or localized stablecoin de-peg distortion.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **FREEZE NEW ENTRIES & SUPPRESS VENUE-SPECIFIC STOP TRIGGERS** until cross-venue dispersion compresses back below `35 bps` across `>= 3` venues.

### CSU-36C: Determinism Replay or Rule-Hash Drift Violation ("Governance Halt")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `ruleset_yaml_sha256` does not match the version-controlled GitHub configuration hash, **OR** deterministic replay of `input_snapshot_sha256` fails to reproduce the logged `MOD 18 / MOD 33` verdict bit-for-bit.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** Unlogged manual override, schema-adapter mismatch, or race condition compromised audit reproducibility.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **IMMEDIATE EXECUTION HALT (`0.00%` New Risk).** Route snapshot to `MOD 28` Live Autopsy and require manual operator hash reconciliation before re-arming.

### CSU-36D: Full Telemetry Attestation Passed (`TRUST_VERIFIED = GREEN`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. All critical feeds fresh (`<= 2.0x` SLA), `>= 3` venues within `<= 35 bps` quorum, NTP clock skew `< 2000 ms`, and `ruleset_yaml_sha256` verified.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** Input state is verified, reproducible, and safe for downstream `MOD 33` regime evaluation.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **EMIT `MOD_36_STATE = GREEN` (`TRUST_VERIFIED`).** Pass control downstream to `MOD 33` Master Radar (`GREEN` does not raise risk above `0.75%`).

---

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **M1: Top-of-Hour GitHub Cron Delay** | `GREEN` (Hourly Cron) | Scheduled `:05` UTC GitHub Actions job queues for `> 45 min` during peak load, pushing heartbeat gap past `90 min`. | Pipeline running on stale prior-hour snapshot. | **Flip to `UNKNOWN` at `90 min` mark**; require `2` consecutive clean hourly cycles to re-arm. |
| **M2: Secondary Feed Missing, Core Fresh** | `GREEN` | Core price/CVD/depth feeds are fresh (`< 5 min`), **but a slow macro/on-chain feed (`e.g., H.4.1` or `Farside`) is delayed**. | Core execution tape is valid; slow background feed is pending publication. | Keep core state `AMBER` (do not freeze exits), apply `MOD 31 M7` 50% caution penalty to the missing macro/on-chain sub-metric. |
| **M3: Stablecoin De-Peg Quorum Split** | `GREEN` (3-Venue Quorum) | Coinbase USD and Kraken USD agree within `8 bps`, **but Binance USDT trades `+45 bps` higher** due to USDT de-peg pressure. | Cross-venue dispersion caused by quote-currency (`USDT` vs `USD`) divergence, not API failure. | Exclude USDT venue from USD reference price; anchor execution exclusively to **Domestic USD quorum (`MOD 07`)**. |
| **M4: Post-Outage Re-Arm Verification** | `UNKNOWN` (Frozen) | Outage resolves and fresh telemetry arrives on Cycle 1. | Single packet could still contain catch-up backlog spikes. | **Hold Freeze Through Cycle 1;** lift `UNKNOWN` lock only after **Cycle 2** passes all SLA & quorum checks. |
| **M5: Manual Operator Override Logged** | Any Active State | Operator manually overrides a `MOD 33` or `MOD 18` rule during live trading. | Discretionary intervention bypassed deterministic ruleset. | **Force `override_audit_flag = TRUE`** in JSON log; mandate `MOD 28` post-trade autopsy regardless of P&L outcome. |
| **M6: Local VPS / Mobile Clock Drift** | `GREEN` | System clock drifts `> 2000 ms` from UTC NTP reference. | Candle-close timestamps (`1H / 4H` bar-count rules in `00C`) could misalign across exchanges. | Trigger `AMBER` clock-sync warning; block bar-close entries until NTP sync restores `< 500 ms`. |
| **M7: Schema Adapter Unmapped Key Spike** | `GREEN` | `cortex_schema_adapter.py` reports `> 5` newly unmapped critical keys after an upstream API schema change. | Upstream vendor changed JSON field names silently. | **Flip to `UNKNOWN` (`Schema Drift Halt`)** until `KEY_ALIAS_MAP` is updated and unit-tested. |
| **M8: Clean Audit Trail Attestation** | Routine Cycle | Every cycle writes immutable `(input_hash, ruleset_hash, verdict_hash)` to Git history. | Guarantees 100% walk-forward backtest fidelity without look-ahead bias. | Archive `as_of_timestamp_utc` alongside `publication_timestamp_utc` for all delayed macro/ETF prints. |

<a id="mod_37__executable_liquidity_net-30r_slippage___equity-curve"></a>

# [41/44] MOD 37 — Executable Liquidity, Net-3.0R Slippage   Equity-Curve

*Source File: `MOD_37_—_Executable_Liquidity,_Net-3.0R_Slippage___Equity-Curve`*

---

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 37: EXECUTABLE LIQUIDITY, NET-3.0R SLIPPAGE & EQUITY-CURVE GOVERNOR
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 4 & Tier D Control-Plane Execution & Portfolio-Heat Governor (Verifies L2 executable depth, post-cost `Net R >= 3.0`, stressed stop slippage, and temporal drawdown caps).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 40` + Qwen `MOD 40` + Perplexity `MOD 38`) — ensuring theoretical chart setups survive real-world order-book impact, trading fees, stressed stop-loss slippage, and multi-trade temporal loss limits without ever exceeding the **0.75% Sovereign Risk Shield**.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_37"
entry_type: "EXECUTABLE_LIQUIDITY_AND_EQUITY_GOVERNOR"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_4_TIER_D_EXECUTION_AND_HEAT_GOVERNOR"
title: "Executable Liquidity, Net-3.0R Slippage & Equity-Curve Governor"

# SUPREME GOVERNANCE PRIORITY
governance_priority: 2 # Sits directly beneath MOD 36 Trust Gate and above MOD 18 Order Entry
advisory_invariant: "Advisory & hard-veto decision governor only; never initiates autonomous orders or raises risk above 0.75%."

# EXECUTABLE LIQUIDITY & POST-COST 3.0R FORMULAS
execution_cost_equations:
  r_planned_usd: "(Position_Size_BTC * Stop_Distance_USD) + Estimated_Roundtrip_Fees_and_Entry_Slippage_USD"
  r_stressed_usd: "(Position_Size_BTC * Stop_Distance_USD) + Stressed_Exit_Slippage_USD + Roundtrip_Fees_USD"
  net_reward_to_risk: "(Target_T2_USD - Expected_Avg_Fill_USD - Roundtrip_Costs_Per_BTC) / (Expected_Avg_Fill_USD - Hard_Stop_USD + Stressed_Slippage_Per_BTC)"

# L2 ORDER BOOK & VENUE HEALTH THRESHOLDS
venue_liquidity_thresholds:
  max_top_of_book_spread_bps: 15.0 # Sustained > 15 bps for 30 min -> DEGRADED / BLOCK
  min_depth_vs_30d_median_pct: 60.0 # Depth down > 40% vs 30D median -> DEGRADED
  max_order_impact_of_10bps_depth_pct: 25.0 # Intended spot order must not consume > 25% of +/-10 bps L2 book depth
  min_net_r_multiple: 3.0 # Hard post-cost asymmetry floor (00C + MOD 18)

# TEMPORAL EQUITY-CURVE & PORTFOLIO HEAT INVARIANTS
equity_governor_limits:
  max_portfolio_heat_pct: 0.75 # Sum of open risk + pending entry risk across ALL positions <= 0.75%
  daily_loss_budget_pct: -1.50 # Day realized + open P&L <= -1.50% (2 full stops) -> 24H FLAT-LOCK
  weekly_loss_budget_pct: -3.00 # Rolling 7D P&L <= -3.00% -> 7-DAY COOLDOWN + MOD 28 Autopsy
  trailing_20_trade_min_avg_winner_r: 2.50 # Below 2.50R over 20 trades -> Strategy Drift Half-Risk (0.375%)

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  amber_reduced_risk_pct: 0.375
  red_lock_risk_pct: 0.00
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Execution & Drawdown Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Post-Cost `Net R >= 3.0` Law (`CB-01`):** A setup showing `3.3R` on a clean chart that degrades to `2.6R` after bid-ask spread, L2 book walk, maker/taker fees, and stressed stop slippage is **mathematically negative-expectancy** under your system. `MOD 37` blocks any entry where `Net_R < 3.0`.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Portfolio-Wide Heat Ceiling (`CB-02`):** Multiple staggered or re-entered tranches (`e.g., T1 + T2`) can never sum to more than **`0.75%` total portfolio equity at risk** (`0.375%` in Amber regimes). If an existing tranche still carries `0.40%` open risk, a secondary add is capped at `0.35%`.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Temporal Loss Budgets (`CB-03`):** Per-trade caps do not stop clustering losses during hostile regime transitions. Hitting **`-1.50%` daily P&L** triggers a mandatory **24-hour `FLAT-LOCK`**, and hitting **`-3.00%` rolling 7-day P&L** enforces a **7-day cooldown + `MOD 28` Autopsy**.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Cold Reserve vs. Execution Float Isolation (`CB-04`):** Operational trading capital is strictly bounded to a prefunded spot execution float; `MOD 37` never assumes instant access to 2-of-3 multi-sig cold reserves (`72-hour operational wall`, `MOD 32`).

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-37A: Executable Depth Degradation or Net-R Collapse (`NEW_ENTRY = BLOCKED`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Top-of-book spread widens above **`15 bps`** for `30+ minutes`, **OR** `1%` L2 book depth drops **`> 40%` below its 30-day median**, **OR** intended spot order size exceeds **`25%` of `+/-10 bps` L2 depth**.
  2. Recomputing entry/stop/target with realistic L2 book walk and fees yields **`Net_R < 3.0`** or **`R_stressed > 0.75%` (`0.375%` Amber)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Execution Lens:** Thin book and adverse selection will eat the theoretical chart edge via entry/exit slippage.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK NEW ENTRY (`MOD_37_STATE = RED`)** or scale down order size / switch strictly to passive post-only limit ladder (`MOD 21`) until `Net_R >= 3.0` and `R_stressed <= approved_risk_budget`.

### CSU-37B: Daily or Weekly Loss Budget Exhausted ("Temporal Drawdown Lock")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Intraday realized + unrealized P&L reaches **`<= -1.50%`** of portfolio equity (`two full 0.75% stops`), **OR** rolling 7-day P&L reaches **`<= -3.00%`**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Governance Lens:** Strategy is out of phase with market microstructure or experiencing a regime transition; continuing to trade risks compounding tilt/whipsaw losses.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE `FLAT-LOCK` (Overrides all `MOD 33` entry permissions):**
    * If Daily `-1.50%` breached: **24-hour hard lock** (`0.00%` new risk).
    * If Weekly `-3.00%` breached: **7-day hard cooldown** + mandatory `MOD 28` Live Autopsy before re-arming.

### CSU-37C: Portfolio Heat Invariant Breach ("Stacked Tranche Cap")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Proposed new order risk plus existing open-position risk (`measured to hard stops`) exceeds **`0.75%` of total equity** (`or 0.375%` under Amber regimes).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Governance Lens:** Stacked re-entries would breach the single-event Sovereign Risk Shield if a sudden gap hits all stops simultaneously.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **REJECT OR TRIM INCREMENTAL ORDER** so `Total_Open_Portfolio_Heat <= Max_Approved_Risk_Pct` (`0.75%` or `0.375%`).

### CSU-37D: Realized Expectancy / CUSUM Drift Warning ("Exit-Engine Recalibration")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Over a trailing window of `20` completed trades, realized average winner drops below **`2.50R`** (design target `>= 3.0R`) or cumulative sum (`CUSUM`) of `(R_i - E[R])` breaches control limit `h`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Governance Lens:** Either stops are too tight for current Garman-Klass volatility (`MOD 12`) or Stage 1/Stage 2 exits are cutting winners prematurely.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **THROTTLE MAX RISK TO `0.375%` (`AMBER`)** and trigger `MOD 18` exit-parameter calibration review until `20` validated trades restore expectancy.

---

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **M1: Pre-Event Depth Evaporation** | `GREEN` (Valid Setup) | 15 minutes before a macro print, L2 `1%` bid depth drops `-45%` (`like Nov 13's thin $72M book`), pushing `R_stressed` to `1.15%`. | Stop-loss slippage risk jumped 50% above the `0.75%` ceiling. | **Automatically Scale Down BTC Size by 35%** (or abort) so `R_stressed <= 0.75%`. |
| **M2: Passive Limit Slice Rescue** | `AMBER` (Size > 25% of 10bps book) | Single market/aggressive limit order would walk 18 bps of book, dropping `Net_R` from `3.2R` to `2.7R`. | Market impact destroys `3.0R` asymmetry if executed in one clip. | **Route to `MOD 21` TWAP / Post-Only Ladder** (`3` child slices); approve only if blended `Net_R >= 3.0`. |
| **M3: Breakeven Stop Frees Portfolio Heat** | `CSU-37C` (Heat at `0.75%`) | Open Trade 1 hits Stage 1 (`50%` banked) and its trailing stop moves strictly **above entry price (`risk-free / locked profit`)**. | Open downside risk on Trade 1 is now `0.00%` of equity. | **Reset Trade 1 Open Heat to `0.00%`**, unlocking fresh `0.75%` risk budget for a new setup. |
| **M4: Daily Lock Override Attempt** | `CSU-37B` (`-1.50%` Daily Lock) | A textbook `CONFIG_J` Bear Trap prints 4 hours after hitting `-1.50%` daily loss limit. | High-conviction setup appears during mandatory cooling-off period. | **Hard Veto Preserved:** Never bypass the `24h` daily lock; log missed setup for `MOD 28` review. |
| **M5: Exchange API Rate-Limit / Reject Spike** | `GREEN` | Primary execution venue returns HTTP `429/5xx` errors or order-ack latency exceeds `1500 ms`. | Venue execution reliability degraded; stop/exit updates could fail. | **Set `MOD_37_STATE = RED` (`Venue Degraded`)**; halt new entries until venue API latency normalizes `< 300 ms`. |
| **M6: Stressed Stop Gap Adjustment** | Active Open Swing | Weekend or macro volatility doubles Garman-Klass APR (`MOD 12 M5`), increasing expected stop-out slippage. | Historical 20 bps stop-slippage buffer is now too narrow for live volatility. | **Recompute `R_stressed` with `2.0x` slippage buffer**; trim partial exposure if `R_stressed` exceeds `0.90%`. |
| **M7: Fee-Tier / Zero-Fee Pair Shift** | Routine Audit | Execution venue changes maker/taker fee schedule on `BTC/USD` or `BTC/USDT`. | Roundtrip transaction cost changes `Net_R` threshold calculations. | Update `roundtrip_fee_bps` constant in `btc_config.json` before next live entry evaluation. |
| **M8: Post-Cooldown Half-Risk Ramp** | Exiting `7-Day` Weekly Lock | 7-day cooldown and `MOD 28` autopsy complete cleanly. | Re-entering live markets after a `-3.0%` drawdown requires controlled re-acclimation. | **Cap First 5 Post-Cooldown Trades at `0.375%` Risk** (`Half-Risk`) before restoring full `0.75%` ceiling. |

<a id="mod_38__etf_primary-market_settlement___brrny_fix-window_engin"></a>

# [42/44] MOD 38 — ETF Primary-Market Settlement   BRRNY Fix-Window Engin

*Source File: `MOD_38_—_ETF_Primary-Market_Settlement___BRRNY_Fix-Window_Engin`*

---

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 38: ETF PRIMARY-MARKET SETTLEMENT & BRRNY FIX-WINDOW ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 & Pillar 5 Institutional Settlement Mechanics Engine (Extends `MOD 07` by separating intraday creation/redemption arbitrage during the `15:00–16:00 NY` BRRNY fix window from `T+1` lagged flow tables and in-kind vs. cash AP delivery).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 39` + Qwen `MOD 36` + Meta `MOD 38`) — incorporating the SEC's **July 29, 2025 in-kind ETP creation/redemption approval**, **T+1 settlement mechanics**, and the **CME CF BRRNY (`15:00–16:00 ET`)** daily NAV benchmark window.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_38"
entry_type: "INSTITUTIONAL_ETF_SETTLEMENT_ENGINE"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_2_5_ETF_PRIMARY_MARKET_SETTLEMENT"
title: "ETF Primary-Market Settlement & BRRNY Fix-Window Engine"

# PILLAR 1 MASTER PERMISSION PRE-CHECK
pillar_1_permission_required: true
icdm_integration: "Adjusts MOD 31 Vector 2 Institutional Flow quality (capped +/- 8 pts; never double-counts Farside prints)."

# PRIMARY TELEMETRY INPUTS (INTRADAY NOWCAST VS T+1 SETTLEMENT)
etf_settlement_telemetry:
  tracked_spot_etfs: ["IBIT", "FBTC", "BITB", "ARKB", "GBTC", "BTC"]
  nav_fix_benchmark: "CME CF BRRNY (15:00-16:00 ET / 20:00-21:00 UTC calculation window)"
  intraday_inav_premium_bps: "((ETF_Market_Price / (BTC_Per_Share * Live_BRRNY_Composite_BTC)) - 1.0) * 10000"
  creation_redemption_route: ["CASH_SETTLEMENT", "IN_KIND_DELIVERY", "UNKNOWN"]
  settlement_lag_convention: "T+1 (Trade date order recorded T+0; BTC/cash settled T+1)"

# DETERMINISTIC THRESHOLDS (INITIAL CALIBRATION PARAMETERS)
settlement_thresholds:
  fix_window_premium_bull_bps: 15.0 # >= +15 bps across >= 4 of 6 ETFs during 15:00-16:00 ET
  fix_window_discount_bear_bps: -15.0 # <= -15 bps across >= 4 of 6 ETFs during 15:00-16:00 ET
  session_discount_persistence_pct: 60.0 # >= 60% of US session trading at discount -> AP redemption pressure

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  redemption_headwind_max_risk_pct: 0.375
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (ETF Primary-Market Anti-Trap Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — In-Kind vs. Cash Creation Distinction (`CB-01`):** Following the SEC's **July 29, 2025 approval of in-kind creations and redemptions** for spot Bitcoin ETPs, Authorized Participants (APs) can deliver or receive existing BTC inventory directly without executing a public spot-market buy/sell order. Never assume an in-kind share creation equals fresh public-market USD buying unless confirmed by **Domestic USD Spot CVD (`MOD 05 / MOD 19`)**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — `T+1` Flow Lag Awareness (`CB-02`):** Public ETF flow tables (e.g., Farside Investors) publish after evening share-count updates and reflect **prior-session (`T+0`) orders settling `T+1`**. Never treat an evening Farside print as a live intraday momentum trigger; use **intraday iNAV premium/discount breadth** as the contemporaneous nowcast and Farside prints strictly as `T+1` settlement confirmation.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — The `15:00–16:00 ET` (`20:00–21:00 UTC`) BRRNY Fix-Window Guard (`CB-03`):** Major US spot ETFs (including IBIT) strike daily NAV against the **CME CF BRRNY** window (`15:00–16:00 ET`). Persistent `<= -15 bps` ETF discounts entering `15:00 ET` signal AP redemption arbitrage (selling spot into the fix)—block new spot entries until the `16:00 ET` (`21:00 UTC`) fix completes.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Never Infer Flow From Share Price Alone (`CB-04`):** High ETF share volume with zero iNAV premium/discount represents secondary-market share rotation between equity traders, **not** primary-market BTC creation or redemption.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-38A: BRRNY Fix-Window Creation Arbitrage ("Mechanically-Backed Spot Bid")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. During **`15:00–16:00 ET` (`20:00–21:00 UTC`)**, at least **`4 of 6` major spot ETFs** (`IBIT, FBTC, BITB, ARKB, GBTC, BTC`) trade at a persistent intraday premium **`>= +15 bps`** above indicative NAV (`BTC_per_share * BRRNY_composite`).
  2. Confirmed by **positive Domestic USD Spot CVD (`MOD 05`)** and **Coinbase Premium `>= +0.03%` (`MOD 19`)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** APs are capturing the ETF premium by buying spot BTC across BRRNY constituent venues (Coinbase, Kraken, Bitstamp, Gemini, LMAX) to hedge cash creations into the `16:00 ET` fix.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **AWARD CONFLUENCE CREDIT TO `MOD 18` (`-6 pts` ICDM fragility)**; defer `Stage 1` profit-taking until after the `16:00 ET` (`21:00 UTC`) fix completes, and authorize `0.75%` full-risk long setups if `MOD 33` permits.

### CSU-38B: Persistent Intraday Discount & AP Redemption Nowcast ("Pre-Print Distribution")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. At least **`4 of 6` spot ETFs** trade at an intraday discount **`<= -15 bps`** for **`>= 60%` of the US session** (or into the `15:00–16:00 ET` fix window), **AND** prior `3-day` settled flows are net negative (`< -$250M`).
  2. Confirmed by negative Coinbase Premium (`< -0.05%`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** APs are redeeming ETF shares and unloading spot BTC across constituent venues ahead of tonight's `T+1` outflow report (`MOD 17 M8` NY-close dump).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL NEW SPOT LONGS (`+8 pts` ICDM fragility)**; if holding an active swing long, tighten trailing stop before `20:00 UTC` (`15:00 ET`) and accelerate `Stage 1` exit to VAL loss.

### CSU-38C: In-Kind Creation / Redemption Re-Classification ("Zero Public-Book Impact")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Issuer daily holdings file reports a large share creation or redemption (`>= $200M`), **but intraday iNAV premium/discount stayed flat (`-5 bps to +5 bps`) AND Domestic USD Spot CVD showed zero corresponding impulse**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** Institutional holder executed an **in-kind basket transfer** (`SEC July 2025 rule`), moving existing BTC into/out of the trust wrapper without hitting public spot order books.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **DO NOT CHASE OR PANIC-SELL ON HEADLINE FLOW NUMBER.** Tag flow event as `IN_KIND_NEUTRAL` and keep execution anchored to live `MOD 05 / MOD 30` spot order flow.

### CSU-38D: Post-Redemption Settlement Exhaustion ("T+1 Overhang Cleared")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following `3+` days of heavy redemptions, intraday ETF discounts compress from `-25 bps` back to **`>= 0 bps`**, Domestic USD Spot CVD turns positive, and price reclaims 4H VAL (`MOD 11A`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** AP redemption selling pressure has subsided; secondary demand is absorbing remaining supply.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LIFT REDEMPTION VETO;** permit `0.375%–0.75%` spot reclaim entry subject to `MOD 33` Master Regime and `R >= 3.0`.

---

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **M1: `15:00 ET` Fix Squeeze Ignition** | Neutral US Session | At `15:05 ET (20:05 UTC)`, IBIT + FBTC premiums jump from `+4 bps` to **`+22 bps`** while Coinbase USD Spot CVD spikes vertical. | Heavy late-day institutional cash creation orders hitting the BRRNY fix window. | **Authorize `0.75% Risk` Breakout/Retest Entry** (`MOD 30 CONFIG_A`) if `MOD 33` permits. |
| **M2: `15:00 ET` Redemption Waterfall** | Fragile / `REGIME_3/4` | Entering `15:00 ET (20:00 UTC)`, ETF discounts widen to **`-25 bps`** and spot breaks 1H VAL on expanding sell delta. | APs executing end-of-day redemption basket sales into a thin book (`MOD 17 M8`). | **Enforce Immediate `100% Cash Lock`**; exit any tactical long before `20:15 UTC`. |
| **M3: High ETF Volume, Zero iNAV Spread** | Any Active State | ETF share volume hits `2.0x` 30D average, **but iNAV spread stays `+/- 3 bps` all day**. | Pure secondary-market rotation between equity traders—zero primary-market BTC creation/redemption. | **Ignore Volume Headline;** maintain baseline `MOD 33` regime posture. |
| **M4: T+1 Outflow Print After Green Intraday** | `CSU-38D` (Reclaim) | Evening Farside report prints `-$250M` outflow (from yesterday's `T+0` redemptions), **even though today's intraday iNAV closed at `+12 bps`**. | Lagged `T+1` accounting print reflecting yesterday's already-absorbed selling. | **Do Not Panic-Exit Today's Reclaim;** trust today's live iNAV + USD Spot CVD over `T+1` prints. |
| **M5: Corporate Treasury ATM / Convertible Supply** | `REGIME_2 / 3` | Major corporate BTC proxy (`e.g., MSTR`) announces an active at-the-market (`ATM`) equity/convertible raise alongside ETF inflows. | Corporate treasury purchases frequently execute via OTC/TWAP rather than aggressive market lifting. | Require `MOD 21` TWAP absorption confirmation before assuming immediate breakout follow-through. |
| **M6: Post-In-Kind Regime Coefficient Drift** | Quarterly Calibration | Rolling 60-day regression of `Farside_Flow` vs. `Same_Day_Spot_Return` drops `> 2 sigma` below 2024 cash-only baseline. | In-kind ETP arbitrage permanently dampened spot-impact-per-dollar of reported ETF flows. | **Down-weight Raw Flow Magnitude by 50%** in `MOD 07`; rely primarily on `Coinbase Premium + USD Spot CVD`. |
| **M7: Pre-Market Gap Arbitrage Trap** | `13:30 UTC` NY Open | BTC rallies `+3%` overnight in Asia, causing ETFs to gap up at `09:30 ET` with a `-18 bps` discount to live spot. | US equity open is pricing ETF shares *below* overnight spot—APs will sell spot to arbitrage the gap. | **Block NY Open Chasing (`09:30–10:15 ET`)**; wait for spot and ETF iNAV to converge first. |
| **M8: Issuer Holdings File Delay (`UNKNOWN`)** | Overnight Audit | Custodian/issuer fails to publish updated `BTC_per_share` or shares-outstanding file by `08:00 MYT`. | `iNAV` denominator cannot be verified cleanly (`MOD 36`). | Tag `MOD_38_STATE = UNKNOWN` until issuer basket file updates; do not extrapolate prior-day shares. |

# [43/44] MOD 39 — Exchange Supply Activation   Aged-UTXO Dormancy Engine

*Source File: `MOD_39_—_Exchange_Supply_Activation___Aged-UTXO_Dormancy_Engine`*

---

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 39: EXCHANGE SUPPLY ACTIVATION & AGED-UTXO DORMANCY ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 5 On-Chain Supply-Activation Engine (Tracks dormant/aged UTXO spending and entity-adjusted BTC migration onto exchange-controlled infrastructure *before* realized spot selling prints).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 37` + Qwen `MOD 38` + Meta `MOD 39`) — separating *cohort valuation* (`MOD 13–15`) from *exchange-accessible supply activation* (`Supply-Adjusted CDD`, `Dormancy`, and `Point-in-Time Entity-Adjusted Exchange Net Position Change`) with a strict anti-double-counting cap against `MOD 15`.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_39"
entry_type: "ONCHAIN_SUPPLY_ACTIVATION_ENGINE"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_5_EXCHANGE_SUPPLY_ACTIVATION"
title: "Exchange Supply Activation & Aged-UTXO Dormancy Engine"

# PILLAR 1 & ANTI-DOUBLE-COUNTING GOVERNANCE
pillar_1_permission_required: true
anti_double_count_rule: "ICDM Vector 4 applies max(MOD_15_LTH_Delta, MOD_39_Supply_Delta) — NEVER sum both deltas."
point_in_time_integrity: "Historical backtests MUST use point-in-time entity-adjusted exchange labels (never retroactively relabeled clusters)."

# PRIMARY TELEMETRY INPUTS (SUPPLY ACTIVATION & VENUE MIGRATION)
supply_activation_telemetry:
  supply_adjusted_cdd_zscore: "Z-score of (Value_BTC * Coin_Lifespan_Days) normalized by circulating supply"
  dormancy_7d_trend: "CDD / Transfer_Volume_BTC (average age of spent coins)"
  entity_adjusted_exchange_netflow_72h_btc: "Confirmed external inflows minus outflows across entity-labelled exchanges (excludes internal wallet reshuffles)"
  exchange_reserve_30d_change_pct: "30-day percentage change in total BTC held on identified exchanges"
  illiquid_supply_30d_change_btc: "30-day net change in entities with < 25% historical spending ratio"
  exchange_label_confidence: "Attribution confidence score (HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW)"

# DETERMINISTIC THRESHOLDS (INITIAL CALIBRATION PARAMETERS)
supply_thresholds:
  cdd_spike_zscore_min: 3.0 # Supply-adjusted CDD >= +3.0 sigma -> Aged UTXO awakening
  net_inflow_72h_overhang_btc: 12000.0 # Sustained 72h entity-adjusted net exchange inflow
  supply_shock_outflow_30d_pct: -2.0 # Exchange balances down > -2.0% over 30D -> Structural supply sink

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  supply_overhang_max_risk_pct: 0.375
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (On-Chain Supply Anti-Noise Rules)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Exchange Deposit $\neq$ Immediate Market Sell (`CB-01`):** Coins moving into exchange wallets may be intended for OTC settlement, derivatives collateral, or institutional custody rather than immediate spot liquidation. An exchange inflow spike **never** triggers a standalone market exit unless corroborated by deteriorating spot structure (`Spot < STH Realized Price`, `USD Spot CVD < 0`, or `4H POC/VAL rejection`).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Entity-Adjustment Mandatory (`CB-02`):** Raw on-chain transfer volume and unadjusted Coin Days Destroyed (`CDD`) are heavily distorted by internal exchange cold-to-hot wallet consolidations. Only **entity-adjusted** exchange flows and **supply-adjusted CDD** may enter `MOD 39`.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Anti-Double-Counting Cap vs. `MOD 15` (`CB-03`):** Because `MOD 15` already tracks LTH-to-STH wealth transfer, `MOD 31` ICDM takes **`max(MOD_15_points, MOD_39_points)`**—preventing the same old-coin distribution wave from being penalized twice.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Low Label Confidence Quarantine (`CB-04`):** If an on-chain provider flags `exchange_label_confidence == LOW` (unidentified cluster migration), tag `MOD_39_STATE = UNKNOWN` (`MOD 36`) rather than triggering a false distribution alert.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-39A: Aged-UTXO Awakening & Exchange Supply Overhang ("Pre-Distribution Warning")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Supply-Adjusted CDD `>= +3.0 sigma`** (dormant `1Y+` UTXOs awakening) **AND** **72-hour entity-adjusted net exchange inflow `> +12,000 BTC`** (`HIGH` label confidence).
  2. **Structural Confirmation:** Price trades below `STH Realized Price` (`MOD 13`, `STH-MVRV < 1.00`) OR fails to hold above `4H POC / VAH` with weakening `USD Spot CVD` (`MOD 05`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Dormant holders have moved physical inventory onto exchange order books into weak spot demand—creating overhead supply overhang.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK NEW SPOT LONGS (`+7 pts` ICDM fragility, subject to `max(MOD 15, MOD 39)` cap)**; if holding an active swing long, **accelerate `Stage 1 (50%)` profit exit** on the first 4H close below POC.

### CSU-39B: Structural Supply Sink & Re-Dormancy ("Illiquid Squeeze Tailwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **30-day exchange reserves drop `<= -2.0%`** (persistent entity-adjusted net exchange outflows for `>= 7 consecutive days`) **AND** **illiquid supply increases** over 30 days.
  2. **Supply-Adjusted CDD & Dormancy** remain at or below baseline (`Z < +1.0`), proving long-term holders are locking coins away rather than spending.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Available exchange float is contracting; shallow pullbacks get absorbed quickly once `MOD 33` permits trend entries.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **AWARD CONFLUENCE CREDIT TO `MOD 18` (`-6 pts` ICDM fragility)**; allow `Stage 2` trailing runners extra room (`hold through first pullback above 4H POC`) when `MOD 33` is in `REGIME_1` or `REGIME_2`.

### CSU-39C: Internal Custodian / Exchange Consolidation ("False Inflow Filter")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Raw CDD or gross transfer volume spikes, **but entity-adjusted net exchange inflow stays `~0 BTC`** (coins moved within the same custodian or exchange entity).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Routine operational wallet maintenance—zero net increase in sellable exchange inventory.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **SUPPRESS SUPPLY ALERT (`MOD_39_STATE = GREEN / NEUTRAL`).** Zero change to active risk or exit posture.

### CSU-39D: Post-Distribution Inflow Exhaustion ("Overhang Absorbed")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following a `CSU-39A` inflow spike, 72H entity-adjusted net exchange flows flip back negative (`net outflows`), `Supply-Adjusted CDD` drops `< +1.0 sigma`, and price reclaims 4H VAL/POC with positive `USD Spot CVD`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Deposited supply has been absorbed by buyers without breaking structural support.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LIFT SUPPLY OVERHANG VETO;** re-enable standard `MOD 18 / MOD 33` entry evaluation (`R >= 3.0`).

---

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **M1: Whale Deposit + Perp Pump Divergence** | `REGIME_1 / 2` (Rally) | Price makes a marginal new high on perp CVD, **while `Supply-Adjusted CDD >= +3.5 Z` and `> 5,000 BTC` hits exchanges** (`MOD 30 CONFIG_H`). | Old coins are moving onto exchanges specifically to sell into retail breakout FOMO. | **Immediate `Stage 1 (50%–70%)` Profit Exit** ahead of visible price breakdown. |
| **M2: Capitulation Inflow Into Passive Bid Wall** | `REGIME_4 / 5` (Selloff) | Heavy `STH` exchange inflows print during a flush, **but `1Y+` LTH CDD stays dormant and `USD Spot CVD` turns sharply positive at HTF VAL**. | Weak hands are panic-depositing into strong institutional absorption (`MOD 06A`). | **Do Not Panic-Sell;** prepare `MOD 30 CONFIG_J` reclaim watch once inflows peak. |
| **M3: Post-Halving / ETF Custody Cold Sweep** | `REGIME_2` (Range) | `> 15,000 BTC` leaves Coinbase/BitGo in a single 48H window while `Dormancy` stays low. | Institutional ETF or corporate treasury cold-storage withdrawal—removing liquid float. | **Bullish Structural Bias (`CSU-39B`)**; prioritize long setups upon 4H VAH acceptance. |
| **M4: Retroactive Cluster Relabeling Guard** | Backtest / Audit | On-chain vendor updates wallet labels retroactively for a past month. | Using retroactively updated labels introduces look-ahead bias into historical calibration. | **Enforce Point-in-Time Snapshot Hash (`MOD 36`)**; never re-score live rules on revised history. |
| **M5: Miner vs. Non-Miner Inflow Attribution** | `CSU-39A` (Inflow Spike) | Entity attribution reveals `> 60%` of the exchange inflow originated from known mining pools rather than dormant investors. | Supply pressure is miner-treasury driven rather than LTH distribution. | **Route Signal to `MOD 40` (Miner Economics)** to avoid double-counting the same BTC transfer. |
| **M6: Multi-Week Exchange Balance Trend Flip** | `CSU-39B` (Supply Sink) | After 60 days of declining exchange balances, 30D exchange reserve change flips from **`-2.5%` to `+1.5%`** while price stalls below 200D MA. | Multi-month accumulation regime has transitioned into net exchange distribution. | **Downgrade `MOD_39_STATE` from `GREEN` to `AMBER`**; tighten trailing stops on open swings. |
| **M7: Stablecoin + BTC Simultaneous Inflow** | `REGIME_2 / 5` | Both `BTC` and `> $500M` in fresh `USDC/USDT` enter spot exchanges simultaneously while `USD Spot CVD` rises. | Fresh stablecoin buying power matches incoming BTC supply (`balanced liquidity expansion`). | **Neutralize Supply Veto;** allow `MOD 30` order flow to arbitrate direction. |
| **M8: On-Chain Indexer Lag (`> 6 Blocks`)** | Routine Scan | Primary on-chain API lags Bitcoin chain tip by `> 6 blocks` (`~60+ min`). | Recent exchange deposit bursts may not yet appear in the JSON feed. | Tag `MOD_39_STATE = UNKNOWN` (`MOD 36`) during high-volatility cascades until indexer catches tip. |
